`dev/gate.mjs`:

```mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// One command that runs every gate the Lead cares about, in order, and prints a
// single verdict. Each stage is an independent process so a hang in one cannot
// stall the others.
//
//   node dev/gate.mjs            all stages
//   node dev/gate.mjs --fast     skip the two stages that spawn REA
//
// Exit: 0 everything green · 1 any hard failure · 2 only warnings

import { execFile } from 'node:child_process'
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

import { locateLayout } from './_layout.mjs'

const HERE = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url))
const { repo: REPO, workspace: ROOT } = locateLayout(HERE)
const argv = process.argv.slice(2)
const fast = argv.includes('--fast')

const run = (label, args, cwd, timeout) => new Promise((done) => {
  const started = Date.now()
  execFile(process.execPath, args, { cwd, timeout, maxBuffer: 32 * 1024 * 1024, windowsHide: true }, (error, stdout, stderr) => {
    done({
      label,
      code: error && typeof error.code === 'number' ? error.code : (error ? 1 : 0),
      out: String(stdout || '') + String(stderr || ''),
      ms: Date.now() - started,
    })
  })
})

/** Last meaningful line of a stage's output, so the summary stays readable. */
const tail = (text) => {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean)
  const checks = lines.find((l) => /checks ·/.test(l))
  const ok = lines.find((l) => /^OK: /.test(l))
  const fail = lines.filter((l) => /^(FAIL|✖)/.test(l)).slice(0, 3)
  return [ok, checks, ...fail].filter(Boolean).join(' | ') || lines[lines.length - 1] || ''
}

// Stage order: the cheap structural gate first, then the live-route probe, then
// the package's own tests. `node --test` with no path discovers test/**/*.test.mjs.
const stages = []
stages.push(['acceptance', await run('acceptance', [join(HERE, 'acceptance.mjs'), ...(fast ? ['--skip-mcp'] : [])], ROOT, 240000)])
stages.push(['http routes', await run('http routes', [join(HERE, 'http-probe.mjs'), ...(fast ? [] : ['--doctor'])], ROOT, 240000)])
stages.push(['unit tests', await run('unit tests', ['--test'], REPO, 300000)])

console.log('')
console.log('================ gate ================')
let fail = 0
let warn = 0
for (const [name, result] of stages) {
  const state = result.code === 0 ? 'PASS' : (result.code === 2 || result.code === 3) ? 'WARN' : 'FAIL'
  if (state === 'FAIL') fail++
  if (state === 'WARN') warn++
  console.log(state + '  ' + name.padEnd(12) + 'exit=' + result.code + '  ' + (result.ms / 1000).toFixed(1) + 's  ' + tail(result.out))
}
console.log('=====================================')
console.log(fail === 0 ? (warn === 0 ? 'ALL GREEN' : 'GREEN with ' + warn + ' warning(s)') : fail + ' stage(s) failed')
process.exit(fail > 0 ? 1 : warn > 0 ? 2 : 0)

```

`dev/http-probe.mjs`:

```mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Fake-ctx probe for the dsh-rea-powerpack host half (lib/index.js).
//
// It imports the plugin exactly the way the DSH loader does — a module whose
// exports carry { name, inject, apply } — but hands it a stub context instead of
// a live harness, then drives the HTTP handler with synthetic node req/res
// objects. Nothing here talks to the real profile: REA_POWERPACK_HOME is
// redirected to a scratch directory so even a write route cannot touch the
// user's engine tree.
//
// Usage: node dev/http-probe.mjs [--doctor] [--repo <dir>]
// Exit: 0 all checks pass · 1 a check failed · 3 usage/plugin missing

import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'

import { locateRepo } from './_layout.mjs'

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url))
const argv = process.argv.slice(2)
const flag = (n) => argv.includes('--' + n)
const opt = (n, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf('--' + n); return i >= 0 && argv[i + 1] ? argv[i + 1] : d }

const repo = resolve(opt('repo', locateRepo(here)))
const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'rea-powerpack-probe-'))
process.env.REA_POWERPACK_HOME = scratch

const results = []
const record = (ok, name, detail) => {
  results.push({ ok, name, detail: detail === undefined ? '' : String(detail) })
  console.log((ok ? 'PASS  ' : 'FAIL  ') + name + (detail === undefined || detail === '' ? '' : '   ' + String(detail)))
}

const entry = join(repo, 'lib', 'index.js')
let mod
try {
  mod = await import(pathToFileURL(entry).href)
} catch (error) {
  if (error && (error.code === 'ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND' || /Cannot find module/.test(String(error.message)))) {
    console.log('SKIP: lib/index.js is not on disk yet (' + entry + ')')
    process.exit(3)
  }
  console.error('FAIL: lib/index.js threw while importing: ' + (error && error.stack ? error.stack : error))
  process.exit(1)
}

record(mod.name === 'dsh-rea-powerpack', 'exports.name', mod.name)
record(Array.isArray(mod.inject) && mod.inject.includes('webServer'), 'exports.inject has webServer', JSON.stringify(mod.inject))
record(typeof mod.apply === 'function', 'exports.apply is a function')
const defaultExport = mod.default && typeof mod.default === 'object' ? mod.default : null
record(defaultExport !== null && defaultExport.apply === mod.apply, 'default export mirrors apply')

// A stub context: only the services a host half is allowed to touch.
const registered = []
const logs = []
const ctx = {
  webServer: {
    register(spec) {
      registered.push(spec)
      return () => {
        const i = registered.indexOf(spec)
        if (i >= 0) registered.splice(i, 1)
      }
    },
  },
  effect(fn, label) {
    const dispose = fn()
    logs.push('effect:' + (label === undefined ? '' : label))
    return typeof dispose === 'function' ? dispose : () => {}
  },
  loader: { entries: () => [] },
  logger: { info() {}, warn() {}, error() {} },
  get: () => undefined,
  profileContext: { profileDir: join(scratch, 'profile'), home: scratch },
  dshHomePath: (...parts) => join(scratch, ...parts),
}

try {
  mod.apply(ctx)
} catch (error) {
  record(false, 'apply(ctx) does not throw', error && error.message)
  process.exit(1)
}
record(registered.length >= 1, 'apply registers a webServer route', registered.length + ' registration(s)')
const mount = registered.find((spec) => spec && typeof spec.handler === 'function')
if (!mount) {
  record(false, 'route registration carries a handler')
  process.exit(1)
}
record(mount.kind === 'prefix', 'route kind is prefix', mount.kind)
record(String(mount.path).replace(/\/+$/, '') === '/rea-powerpack', 'route path is /rea-powerpack', mount.path)

const call = async (method, route, body, remoteAddress) => {
  const chunks = []
  const response = {
    statusCode: 0,
    headers: {},
    setHeader(k, v) { this.headers[String(k).toLowerCase()] = v },
    getHeader(k) { return this.headers[String(k).toLowerCase()] },
    writeHead(status, headers) { this.statusCode = status; if (headers) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headers)) this.setHeader(k, v) },
    write(chunk) { if (chunk !== undefined) chunks.push(Buffer.isBuffer(chunk) ? chunk : Buffer.from(String(chunk))) },
    end(chunk) {
      if (chunk !== undefined && typeof chunk !== 'function') chunks.push(Buffer.isBuffer(chunk) ? chunk : Buffer.from(String(chunk)))
      this.finished = true
    },
    finished: false,
  }
  const payload = body === undefined ? '' : JSON.stringify(body)
  const request = {
    method,
    url: mount.path + route,
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'content-length': String(Buffer.byteLength(payload)) },
    socket: { remoteAddress: remoteAddress === undefined ? '127.0.0.1' : remoteAddress },
    on(event, handler) {
      if (event === 'data' && payload !== '') handler(Buffer.from(payload))
      if (event === 'end') handler()
      return request
    },
    async *[Symbol.asyncIterator]() { if (payload !== '') yield Buffer.from(payload) },
  }
  await mount.handler(request, response)
  const text = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8')
  let json = null
  try { json = JSON.parse(text) } catch {}
  return { status: response.statusCode, headers: response.headers, text, json }
}

// ---- 1. loopback gate -------------------------------------------------------
const denied = await call('GET', '/state', undefined, '10.1.2.3')
record(denied.status === 403, 'non-loopback request is refused', 'status ' + denied.status)

// ---- 2. unknown route -------------------------------------------------------
const missing = await call('GET', '/definitely-not-a-route')
record(missing.status === 404, 'unknown route answers 404', 'status ' + missing.status)
record(missing.json !== null && missing.json.error && typeof missing.json.error.code === 'string', 'unknown route carries error.code', missing.json && missing.json.error ? missing.json.error.code : missing.text.slice(0, 80))

// ---- 3. GET /state ----------------------------------------------------------
const state = await call('GET', '/state')
record(state.status === 200, 'GET /state answers 200', 'status ' + state.status)
const s = state.json
record(s !== null && typeof s === 'object', 'GET /state answers JSON')
if (s && typeof s === 'object') {
  record(Array.isArray(s.engines), 'state.engines is an array', Array.isArray(s.engines) ? s.engines.length + ' row(s)' : typeof s.engines)
  record(s.rea !== null && typeof s.rea === 'object', 'state.rea is an object')
  record(s.settings !== null && typeof s.settings === 'object', 'state.settings is an object')
  record(s.mcp !== null && typeof s.mcp === 'object', 'state.mcp is an object')
  record(s.host !== null && typeof s.host === 'object', 'state.host is an object')
  record(typeof s.writable === 'boolean', 'state.writable is a boolean', s.writable)
  const allowed = new Set(['ok', 'external_ok', 'missing', 'broken', 'unsupported_host', 'requires_dependency', 'external_missing'])
  const bad = (s.engines || []).filter((row) => !allowed.has(row.status)).map((row) => row.id + ':' + row.status)
  record(bad.length === 0, 'engine.status vocabulary matches the client', bad.length ? bad.join(',') : 'all rows valid')
  const noId = (s.engines || []).filter((row) => typeof row.id !== 'string' || row.id === '')
  record(noId.length === 0, 'every engine row has an id')
}

// ---- 4. GET /report is markdown, not JSON -----------------------------------
const report = await call('GET', '/report')
record(report.status === 200, 'GET /report answers 200', 'status ' + report.status)
record(!/^\s*[\[{]/.test(report.text), 'GET /report is not a JSON document', report.text.slice(0, 60).replace(/\n/g, '\\n'))
record(String(report.headers['content-type'] || '').includes('text/markdown') || report.text.includes('#'), 'GET /report looks like markdown')

// ---- 5. POST /settings round-trip ------------------------------------------
const write = await call('POST', '/settings', { updatePolicy: 'manual' })
if (flag('verbose')) console.log('  settings body: ' + JSON.stringify(write.json).slice(0, 400))
record(write.status === 200, 'POST /settings answers 200', 'status ' + write.status)
const after = await call('GET', '/state')
const policy = after.json && after.json.settings ? after.json.settings.updatePolicy : undefined
record(policy === 'manual', 'POST /settings persists updatePolicy', 'policy=' + policy)

// ---- 6. serialisation guard: two concurrent mutating calls ----------------
const [a, b] = await Promise.all([call('POST', '/engines/verify', {}), call('POST', '/engines/verify', {})])
if (flag('verbose')) console.log('  verify bodies: ' + JSON.stringify([a.json, b.json]).slice(0, 700))
const busy = [a, b].filter((r) => r.json && r.json.error && r.json.error.code === 'busy')
record(a.status < 500 && b.status < 500, 'concurrent verify does not 500', 'statuses ' + a.status + '/' + b.status)
record(busy.length <= 1, 'concurrent verify is serialised or both succeed', busy.length + ' busy refusal(s)')

// ---- 7. optional live doctor ----------------------------------------------
if (flag('doctor')) {
  const doctor = await call('GET', '/doctor')
  record(doctor.status === 200, 'GET /doctor answers 200', 'status ' + doctor.status)
  const d = doctor.json
  if (d && typeof d === 'object') {
    record(typeof d.toolCount === 'number' && d.toolCount >= 200, 'doctor reports >= 200 tools', d.toolCount)
    record(typeof d.availableGroups === 'number' && typeof d.totalGroups === 'number', 'doctor reports group counts', d.availableGroups + '/' + d.totalGroups)
  } else {
    record(false, 'GET /doctor answers JSON', doctor.text.slice(0, 120))
  }
}

rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true })

const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.ok).length
console.log('')
console.log(results.length + ' checks · ' + (results.length - failed) + ' pass · ' + failed + ' fail')
process.exit(failed === 0 ? 0 : 1)

```

`dev/install-plugin.mjs`:

```mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Install / uninstall dsh-rea-powerpack into a DSH profile (development link mode).
//
// What it does (and nothing else):
//   1. back up profile/package.json, profile/cordis.patch.yml, profile/pnpm-lock.yaml
//   2. `pnpm add "link:<repo>" --save-exact` in the profile (live junction: edits in the
//      repo are visible without reinstalling)
//   3. add "dsh-rea-powerpack" to dsh.profile.bundles and drop "dsh-rea-agents" from it
//      (both bundles mount an MCP client row named `rea`; running both would collide)
//   4. strip stale `- id: rea-agents*` entries from profile/cordis.patch.yml, keeping them
//      in the backup file
//
// A restart of the harness is required afterwards: the bundle list and patch layers are
// composed once at boot (see @deepseek-ai/dsh-app-boot profile-boot).
//
// Usage:
//   node dev/install-plugin.mjs [--profile <dir>] [--repo <dir>] [--dry-run] [--uninstall]

import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import { copyFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { homedir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';

import { locateRepo } from './_layout.mjs';

const execFileAsync = promisify(execFile);
const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const defaultRepo = locateRepo(here);

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const flag = (n) => argv.includes('--' + n);
const opt = (n, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf('--' + n); return i >= 0 && argv[i + 1] ? argv[i + 1] : d; };

const dryRun = flag('dry-run');
const uninstall = flag('uninstall');
const profile = resolve(opt('profile', join(homedir(), '.dsh', 'profiles', 'desktop')));
const repo = resolve(opt('repo', defaultRepo));
const pkgName = 'dsh-rea-powerpack';
const oldBundle = 'dsh-rea-agents';

const log = (...a) => console.log(...a);
const die = (msg) => { console.error('ERROR: ' + msg); process.exit(1); };

if (!existsSync(join(profile, 'package.json'))) die('not a dsh profile (no package.json): ' + profile);
if (!existsSync(join(repo, 'package.json'))) die('package not found: ' + repo);
const meta = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(repo, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
if (meta.name !== pkgName) die('unexpected package name ' + meta.name);

// ---------- backup ----------
const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
const backupDir = join(profile, '.rea-powerpack-backup', stamp);
const files = ['package.json', 'cordis.patch.yml', 'pnpm-lock.yaml'];
if (!dryRun) {
  mkdirSync(backupDir, { recursive: true });
  for (const f of files) {
    const src = join(profile, f);
    if (existsSync(src)) copyFileSync(src, join(backupDir, f));
  }
}
log('backup dir:', dryRun ? '(dry-run, skipped)' : backupDir);

const readJson = (p) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
const writeJson = (p, v) => writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(v, null, 2) + '\n');
const pkgPath = join(profile, 'package.json');
const patchPath = join(profile, 'cordis.patch.yml');

// ---------- pnpm ----------
// Windows has no POSIX exec target for pnpm: the installed entry point is a
// console script (pnpm.cmd / pnpm.ps1), and Node refuses to spawn a .cmd
// without a shell. The harness also ships its own pnpm as a .mjs entry point.
// Both shapes are handled explicitly so no argument is ever re-parsed by a
// shell: cmd.exe receives the argv array, and a .mjs runs under this Node.
const pnpmInvocation = (args) => {
  const configured = process.env.DSH_PNPM_PATH
  const command = typeof configured === 'string' && configured.trim() !== '' ? configured.trim() : 'pnpm'
  if (/\.[cm]?js$/u.test(command)) return { file: process.execPath, args: [command, ...args] }
  if (process.platform === 'win32') return { file: process.env.ComSpec || 'cmd.exe', args: ['/d', '/s', '/c', command, ...args] }
  return { file: command, args }
}

const pnpm = async (args) => {
  log('$ pnpm ' + args.join(' '));
  if (dryRun) return;
  const invocation = pnpmInvocation(args);
  try {
    const { stdout, stderr } = await execFileAsync(invocation.file, invocation.args, {
      cwd: profile, timeout: 20 * 60_000, maxBuffer: 32 * 1024 * 1024, windowsHide: true, shell: false,
    });
    if (stdout.trim()) log(stdout.trim().split('\n').slice(-12).join('\n'));
    if (stderr.trim()) log(stderr.trim().split('\n').slice(-6).join('\n'));
  } catch (e) {
    die('pnpm ' + args.join(' ') + ' failed: ' + (e.stderr || e.message));
  }
};

// ---------- bundles ----------
const setBundles = (pkg, add, remove) => {
  pkg.dsh ??= {};
  pkg.dsh.profile ??= {};
  const list = Array.isArray(pkg.dsh.profile.bundles) ? pkg.dsh.profile.bundles.slice() : [];
  const next = list.filter((b) => b !== remove && b !== add);
  if (add) next.push(add);
  pkg.dsh.profile.bundles = next;
  return next;
};

// ---------- stale rea-agents patch entries ----------
// The dsh-rea-agents bundle inserted its own mcp-client row with serverName
// "rea". Two live mcp-client instances cannot share a serverName (the host
// throws "serverName \"rea\" is already in use by another mcp-client
// instance"). Once the bundle leaves dsh.profile.bundles, that profile-level
// row has no bundle layer left to supply its config — a config-less mcp-client
// row is not a valid row at all — so the block is REMOVED rather than
// disabled. The removal is idempotent and stays byte-faithful everywhere else.
//
// Block shape:
//   - id: rea-agents-mcp
//     name: "@deepseek-ai/dsh-mcp-client"
//     disabled: false
// (possibly indented under a list; trailing blank line is collapssed).
const stripReaAgentsEntries = (text) => {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
  const out = [];
  let removed = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const match = /^(\s*)- id:\s*(rea-agents\S*)\s*$/.exec(lines[i]);
    if (!match) { out.push(lines[i]); continue; }
    let j = i + 1;
    while (j < lines.length && /^\s+\S/.test(lines[j]) && !/^\s*-\s/.test(lines[j])) j++;
    // Swallow the blank separator line that followed the block, if any.
    if (j < lines.length && lines[j].trim() === '' && out.length > 0 && out[out.length - 1].trim() === '') j++;
    removed++;
    i = j - 1;
  }
  return { text: out.join('\n'), removed };
};

const run = async () => {
  const pkg = readJson(pkgPath);
  const patch = existsSync(patchPath) ? readFileSync(patchPath, 'utf8') : '';
  const { text: cleaned, removed } = stripReaAgentsEntries(patch);
  if (removed) log('removing ' + removed + ' stale dsh-rea-agents patch entr' + (removed === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies') + ' (no bundle layer supplies their config)');

  if (uninstall) {
    await pnpm(['remove', pkgName]);
    // pnpm rewrites package.json, so re-read it instead of persisting our stale copy.
    const current = readJson(pkgPath);
    setBundles(current, null, pkgName);
    writeJson(pkgPath, current);
    if (!dryRun) writeFileSync(patchPath, cleaned);
    log('uninstalled. restart the harness to drop the rows.');
    return;
  }

  await pnpm(['add', 'link:' + repo, '--save-exact']);
  // pnpm rewrites package.json (adds the dependency), so bundles must be written
  // onto a FRESH read — persisting the pre-add copy silently drops the dependency.
  const after = readJson(pkgPath);
  const bundles = setBundles(after, pkgName, oldBundle);
  if (!dryRun) {
    writeJson(pkgPath, after);
    writeFileSync(patchPath, cleaned);
  }
  log('bundles now: ' + bundles.join(', '));
  log('');
  log('OK. Restart DeepSeek Harness to load the plugin (bundle list is read at boot).');
  log('After restart: node dev/verify-mcp.mjs   # expects toolCount >= 200');
};

run().catch((e) => die(e.stack || String(e)));

```

`dev/verify-mcp.mjs`:

```mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Independent end-to-end verifier for rea-powerpack.
//
// It does NOT import the plugin's modules (deliberately: an independent check).
// It spawns the real launcher the same way the harness would, performs a raw
// MCP stdio handshake, then asks REA's own `binary_session` tool which tool
// groups are available — that report is the ground truth for "did the engines
// actually get wired up".
//
// Usage:
//   node dev/verify-mcp.mjs [--profile <dir>] [--pkg <dir>] [--json] [--keep]
//
// Exit codes: 0 all good, 1 handshake failed, 2 provider gaps remain, 3 usage.

import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { homedir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const arg = (name, dflt) => {
  const i = argv.indexOf('--' + name);
  return i >= 0 && argv[i + 1] ? argv[i + 1] : dflt;
};
const has = (name) => argv.includes('--' + name);

const profile = arg('profile', join(homedir(), '.dsh', 'profiles', 'desktop'));
const pkg = arg('pkg', join(profile, 'node_modules', 'dsh-rea-powerpack'));
const launcher = arg('launcher', join(pkg, 'lib', 'rea-mcp.mjs'));
// Repeatable --env KEY=VALUE overrides, so this can also probe an engine set against
// any launcher, before or after the plugin is installed.
const envOverrides = {};
for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
  if (argv[i] === '--env' && argv[i + 1]) { const eq = argv[i + 1].indexOf('='); envOverrides[argv[i + 1].slice(0, eq)] = argv[i + 1].slice(eq + 1); }
}
const home = process.env.REA_POWERPACK_HOME || join(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || join(homedir(), 'AppData', 'Local'), 'rea-powerpack');
const manifestPath = join(home, 'engines.json');

const fail = (code, msg) => { console.error('FAIL: ' + msg); process.exit(code); };

if (!existsSync(launcher)) fail(3, 'launcher not found: ' + launcher);

let manifest = null;
if (existsSync(manifestPath)) {
  try { manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { fail(3, 'engines.json is not valid JSON: ' + e.message); }
} else {
  console.warn('WARN: no engines.json at ' + manifestPath + ' — starting with an empty engine set');
}

const child = spawn(process.execPath, [launcher, profile, 'mcp'], {
  cwd: profile,
  env: { ...process.env, ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE: '1', ...envOverrides },
  stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
  windowsHide: true,
});

let stderr = '';
child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { stderr += d.toString(); });

const pending = new Map();
let nextId = 1;
let buf = '';
child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => {
  buf += chunk.toString('utf8');
  let nl;
  while ((nl = buf.indexOf('\n')) >= 0) {
    const line = buf.slice(0, nl).trim();
    buf = buf.slice(nl + 1);
    if (!line) continue;
    let msg;
    try { msg = JSON.parse(line); } catch { continue; }
    if (msg.id !== undefined && pending.has(msg.id)) {
      const { resolve, reject } = pending.get(msg.id);
      pending.delete(msg.id);
      msg.error ? reject(new Error(JSON.stringify(msg.error))) : resolve(msg.result);
    }
  }
});

const request = (method, params, timeoutMs = 60000) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  const id = nextId++;
  const timer = setTimeout(() => { pending.delete(id); reject(new Error('timeout waiting for ' + method)); }, timeoutMs);
  pending.set(id, {
    resolve: (v) => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve(v); },
    reject: (e) => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(e); },
  });
  child.stdin.write(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id, method, params }) + '\n');
});
const notify = (method, params) => child.stdin.write(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', method, params }) + '\n');

const cleanup = () => { try { child.kill('SIGKILL'); } catch {} };
process.on('exit', cleanup);
process.on('SIGINT', () => { cleanup(); process.exit(130); });

const run = async () => {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const init = await request('initialize', {
    protocolVersion: '2025-06-18',
    capabilities: {},
    clientInfo: { name: 'rea-powerpack-verify', version: '0.0.0' },
  });
  notify('notifications/initialized', {});

  const server = init.serverInfo || {};
  const tools = [];
  let cursor;
  do {
    const page = await request('tools/list', cursor ? { cursor } : {}, 120000);
    tools.push(...(page.tools || []));
    cursor = page.nextCursor;
  } while (cursor);

  const handshakeMs = Date.now() - startedAt;
  const out = {
    launcher,
    profile,
    manifestPath,
    server,
    instructions: (init.instructions || '').split('\n')[0].slice(0, 160),
    toolCount: tools.length,
    // Opt-in so the default report stays small; used by the tool-baseline drift check.
    ...(argv.includes('--list-tools') ? { tools: tools.map((tool) => tool.name) } : {}),
    handshakeMs,
    engines: manifest?.engines?.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, version: e.version, verified: e.verified, path: e.path })) ?? [],
  };

  // Ask REA itself which tool groups are unavailable, and why.
  // Shape (verified against live REA 6.8.0): result.tool_availability = {
  //   client_requirements: {...}, groups: [{ names[], surface, effects[], annotations[],
  //   available, reason, remediation, default_mode_available, modes }] }
  if (tools.some((t) => t.name === 'binary_session')) {
    try {
      const res = await request('tools/call', { name: 'binary_session', arguments: {} }, 180000);
      const text = (res.content || []).filter((c) => c.type === 'text').map((c) => c.text).join('\n');
      let parsed = null;
      try { parsed = JSON.parse(text); } catch {}
      const avail = parsed?.result?.tool_availability || parsed?.tool_availability || {};
      const groups = Array.isArray(avail.groups) ? avail.groups : [];
      const isDown = (g) => g.available === false;
      const down = groups.filter(isDown);
      const byReason = {};
      for (const g of down) byReason[g.reason || 'unknown'] = (byReason[g.reason || 'unknown'] || 0) + 1;
      out.availability = {
        clientRequirements: avail.client_requirements || null,
        groups: groups.length,
        available: groups.length - down.length,
        toolsInAvailableGroups: groups.filter((g) => !isDown(g)).reduce((n, g) => n + (g.names?.length || 0), 0),
        byReason,
        unavailable: down.map((g) => ({
          surface: g.surface,
          tools: g.names?.length || 0,
          sample: (g.names || []).slice(0, 4).join(','),
          reason: g.reason,
          remediation: String(g.remediation || '').slice(0, 240),
        })),
      };
    } catch (e) {
      out.availability = { error: String(e.message).slice(0, 300) };
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 2));
  console.error('--- launcher stderr ---\n' + (stderr.trim() || '(empty)'));

  // A real gap is a provider we were supposed to supply on this host but that is
  // missing/broken. target_required resolves itself once a target is opened;
  // unsupported_host cannot be fixed on Windows; JEB is a commercial client we skip.
  const IGNORED_SURFACES = new Set(['jeb-provider']);
  const gaps = (out.availability?.unavailable || []).filter(
    (g) => (g.reason === 'provider_unavailable' || g.reason === 'provider_missing') && !IGNORED_SURFACES.has(g.surface)
  );
  if (out.toolCount < 200) fail(1, 'tool count ' + out.toolCount + ' < 200 — server started but looks wrong');
  if (gaps.length) {
    console.error('GAPS REMAIN (' + gaps.length + '):');
    for (const g of gaps) console.error('  - ' + g.surface + ' [' + g.reason + '] tools=' + g.tools + ' -> ' + g.remediation);
    process.exit(2);
  }
  console.log('OK: ' + out.toolCount + ' tools, ' + (out.availability?.available ?? '?') + '/' + (out.availability?.groups ?? '?') + ' groups available, ' + (out.availability?.toolsInAvailableGroups ?? '?') + ' tools in available groups');
  process.exit(0);
};

run().catch((e) => { console.error(e); console.error(stderr.slice(-2000)); process.exit(1); });
```

`docs/ENGINES.md`:

```md
# 引擎矩阵

REA 是适配层，不是分析引擎本体。它能调用哪些工具，取决于这台 Windows 机器上
装了什么：Ghidra 决定反编译深度，JADX 决定 Android 反编译，WABT 决定 WASM，
frida 决定运行时观测。**dsh-rea-powerpack 的职责就是把这些引擎补齐、盯住、并在
Settings 卡片里如实汇报。**

本页对应 `lib/catalog.js` 的 `ENGINES` 表与 `lib/engines.js` 的探测/安装逻辑。

## 1. 三个 tier 的含义

| tier | 含义 | 缺失时的后果 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | **基石**：Java、Ghidra。REA 超过一半的原生工具都从这里来 | REA 仍能启动，但原生分析基本不可用；体检里缺失数会直接标红 |
| 1 | **主力**：adb、apktool、jadx、wabt、python、frida | 对应的一类目标（Android / WASM / 运行时）工具整组消失 |
| 2 | **特种**：objdump、rizin、dotnet、ilspy、mitmproxy 等 | 只影响特定格式（.NET、抓包、ELF 反汇编） |

`composeEnv` 只把 **tier ≤ 1 且尚未就绪** 的引擎记为 `missing`——tier 2 缺失不会
阻塞 REA 启动，也不会在概览里制造噪音。

## 2. 引擎矩阵（Windows）

| id | 名称 | tier | 环境变量 | 安装方式 | 备注 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `java` | Java (JDK) | 0 | `JAVA_HOME` | manual | external |
| `ghidra` | Ghidra | 0 | `GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR` | manual | external |
| `adb` | Android Platform Tools (adb) | 1 | `REA_ADB_PATH` | zip | managed |
| `apktool` | Apktool | 1 | `REA_APKTOOL_COMMAND` | github-latest (latest) | managed |
| `jadx` | JADX headless MCP | 1 | `REA_JADX_MCP_JAR` | file | managed |
| `wabt` | WABT (WebAssembly Binary Toolkit) | 1 | `REA_WABT_BIN_DIRECTORY` | zip | managed |
| `python` | Python 3.12 | 1 | PATH | winget | managed |
| `frida` | Frida tools | 1 | PATH | pip | managed |
| `objdump` | GNU objdump | 2 | `REA_OBJDUMP_COMMAND` | manual | external |
| `rizin` | Rizin | 2 | `REA_RIZIN_COMMAND` | manual | external |
| `dotnet` | .NET SDK 8 | 2 | PATH | winget | managed |
| `ilspy` | ILSpy command line (ilspycmd) | 2 | `REA_ILSPY_CMD_PATH` | manual | external |
| `mitmproxy` | mitmproxy (mitmdump) | 2 | `REA_MITMDUMP_COMMAND` | pip | managed |
| `tshark` | TShark | 2 | `REA_TSHARK_COMMAND` | manual | Linux-only in REA |
| `capinfos` | capinfos | 2 | `REA_CAPINFOS_COMMAND` | manual | Linux-only in REA |
| `binwalk` | Binwalk | 2 | `REA_BINWALK_COMMAND` | manual | Linux-only in REA |
| `unblob` | Unblob | 2 | `REA_UNBLOB_COMMAND` | manual | Linux-only in REA |
| `sigrok` | sigrok-cli | 2 | `REA_SIGROK_COMMAND` | manual | Linux-only in REA |
| `pwntools` | pwntools | 2 | `REA_PWNTOOLS_PYTHON` | manual | Linux-only in REA |
| `wakaru` | Wakaru (JavaScript deobfuscation) | 2 | `REA_WAKARU_COMMAND` | manual | Linux-only in REA |
| `process-capture` | Process capture (PTY) | 2 | — | manual | Linux-only in REA |

「managed」指卡片上的**安装 / 卸载**按钮真的会去下载并放进引擎目录；
「external」指需要你自己装好，插件只负责探测与生成环境变量。

## 3. 状态语义

| status | 含义 | 卡片表现 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `ok` | 探测通过，版本可读 | 绿色徽章 + 版本号 |
| `missing` | 引擎目录里没有，或 PATH 上找不到 | 灰色徽章 + 「安装」按钮（可 managed 时） |
| `broken` | 文件在，但 `--version` 探测失败 | 红色徽章 + 「重新安装」 |
| `requires_dependency` | 本体在，但依赖没就绪（典型：JADX 缺 Java） | 黄色徽章 + 依赖提示 |
| `unsupported_host` | REA 本身不支持 Windows 上的这条通路 | 灰色徽章 + 说明；不提供安装，避免装了个用不上的东西 |
| `external_ok` | 外部引擎探测通过（插件不管理它的文件） | 绿色徽章，无卸载按钮 |
| `external_missing` | 外部引擎未检测到 | 黄色徽章 + 手动安装指引 |

**没有裸 `missing` 的 external 引擎。** 一个外部引擎要么 `external_ok`，要么
`external_missing`——这样卡片就不会假装「点一下就能修好」一个它根本不管的东西。

## 4. 引擎目录与环境变量合成

- 默认根目录：`%LOCALAPPDATA%\rea-powerpack\engines`。
  可用 `REA_POWERPACK_HOME` 改状态目录，或在设置里写 `engineRoot`（绝对路径）改引擎目录。
- 每个托管引擎装在自己的子目录里（`engines/<id>/`），清单记在 `engines.json`：
  装机结果（`verified`）才参与环境变量合成，装了一半的不会污染 REA 的环境。
- `composeEnv(home, settings)` 的合成规则，顺序有讲究：

  1. 先放 `record.extraEnv`（引擎自带的附属变量）；
  2. 再放**已验证**记录的 `envVar`：`envKind === 'dir'` 时指向目录，
     `'file'` 时指向文件，未声明时按路径本身；
  3. `pathPrepend` 只收集 `pathPrepend: true` 且已就绪的记录，取文件所在目录，
     **按清单顺序去重**（大小写不敏感），最后整体前置到 `PATH`；
  4. **用户自己设的 `REA_*` 永远优先**——被顶掉的键不会出现在合成结果里，
     而是记进 `REA_POWERPACK_OVERRIDES`（值是被保留的变量名列表），
     这样卡片能明确告诉你「这一项用的是你的设置，不是插件的」。

- 只读部分：插件**不会**改写你的全局环境变量，也不写注册表；所有合成只作用于
  REA 子进程的 `env`。

## 5. 安装顺序与依赖

- `jadx`、`apktool` 需要 Java：先让 `java` 变绿，否则装完仍是 `requires_dependency`。
- `ilspy` 是 `dotnet tool`，先装 `.NET SDK`，再执行
  `dotnet tool install --global ilspycmd`。
- `frida`、`mitmproxy` 走 pip，需要 PATH 上有可用的 Python 3.12；
  卡片的「安装」按钮固定在 `python` 就绪后才建议点击。
- 下载与校验：zip / file 类安装带 `sha256` 的会强制校验，不匹配即
  `checksum_mismatch`，**不会**把半成品写进清单；`github-latest` 只跟随上游最新发布，
  想要可复现就手动固定。

## 6. 探测是怎么做的

`verifyAll` 对每个引擎跑一次轻量探测（`probe`：可执行文件 + 参数 + 可选正则），
默认 20 秒超时，超时算 `broken`。探测只跑 `--version` 这类无副作用命令，
不会启动 Ghidra 的图形界面，也不会替你去连设备。

> 探测会真实执行第三方可执行文件。只安装你信任来源的引擎版本；
> 卡片的每个版本号旁边都能看到落盘路径。

## 7. 维护这张表

新增引擎只需改 `lib/catalog.js`：加一条 `EngineSpec`（id / name / tier /
platforms / envVar / envKind / pathPrepend / probe / install / docs / notes）。
卡片与 `composeEnv` 都从同一张表读取，**不需要改 UI 代码**。

`test/catalog.test.mjs` 会拦住常见错误：id 重复、tier 非法、环境变量命名不合规、
sha256 不是 64 位十六进制、`install.kind` 不认识、`unsupported_host` 缺 notes。

```

`docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md`:

```md
# 故障排查

卡片上的每一条错误都来自宿主返回的稳定错误码（见 ARCHITECTURE §4），
前端只做「码 → 人话」的翻译，不猜原因。下面按「症状 → 先查什么 → 怎么修」组织。

## 0. 三个入口

| 你要什么 | 去哪 |
| --- | --- |
| 一眼看健康度 | 概览：REA 版本 / pin 状态 / 引擎就绪数 / 最近体检时间 / 更新策略 |
| 具体缺什么 | 能力体检：点「运行体检」→ 按 reason 分组 → 每组有修复建议与工具名 |
| 给维护者看 | 维护 → 「导出诊断报告」（Markdown，含状态快照、引擎矩阵、体检缺口、最近日志） |

日志文件：`%LOCALAPPDATA%\rea-powerpack\logs\powerpack.log`（JSONL，一行一个事件）。
状态文件同目录：`engines.json`、`settings.json`、`pin.json`、`state.json`。

## 1. 错误码对照表

| 错误码 | 含义 | 处理 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `loopback_only` | 请求不是从本机 loopback 来的 | 只用 `http://127.0.0.1:...` 访问；不要把 DSH 反代到公网 |
| `not_found` | 路由或引擎 id 不存在 | 检查路径前缀是否 `/rea-powerpack/*`；升级插件到最新 |
| `bad_json` | 请求体不是合法 JSON | 手工调 API 时检查引号/逗号 |
| `bad_request` | 字段不合法（版本号格式、路径为空等） | 版本要 `x.y.z`（可带 `-rc.1`）；路径要绝对路径 |
| `profile_unwritable` | 配置档目录不可写 | 检查 ACL / 磁盘只读 / 杀软拦截；换一个有写权限的 profile |
| `no_engine` | 状态里没有这个引擎记录 | 先「校验全部」，再安装 |
| `unknown_engine` | catalog 里没有这个 id | 用了旧 id 或拼错；看引擎矩阵表 |
| `install_failed` | 下载/解压/安装失败 | 看错误详情里的命令与输出；常见是网络、代理、磁盘满 |
| `download_failed` | HTTP 下载失败 | 检查 `HTTPS_PROXY`；国内网络可先手动下载再「导入」 |
| `checksum_mismatch` | sha256 与 catalog 不符 | **不要继续用**：缓存损坏或被篡改；删掉该引擎目录重装 |
| `unsupported_host` | 该引擎在 Windows 上不被 REA 支持 | 正常现象（tshark/binwalk/sigrok 等），不用修 |
| `requires_dependency` | 本体在，但依赖缺失 | 典型：jadx 缺 Java、ilspy 缺 dotnet；先修依赖 |
| `registry_timeout` | 查新版超时 | 稍后重试；不影响已装的 REA |
| `registry_unreachable` | 连不上 registry | 检查网络/代理/npm registry 配置 |
| `no_such_version` | registry 里没有这个版本 | 核对版本号；prerelease 也要写在版本里 |
| `preflight_failed` | 影子体检没过（版本不符或工具数 < 200） | 该版本已被扣下；查看详情里的 serverVersion/toolCount |
| `rollback_failed` | 升级失败且回滚也失败 | 最严重：按详情手动 `pnpm add rea-agents@<旧版本> --save-exact` |
| `reload_failed` | MCP 行热重载失败 | 重启 DSH；卡片会提示 `restart-required` |
| `pin_conflict` | 已固定到别的版本 | 先解除固定，再指定新版本 |
| `rea_missing` | 配置档里没装 rea-agents | 点「安装」；或确认插件装在正在用的 profile 里 |

未知错误码不会显示成 `undefined`：会原样显示码本身 + 宿主给的 detail。

## 2. 卡片不出现 / tab 是空的

1. 确认插件装进了**正在使用**的 profile（`dsh plugin --profile web ls`）；
2. 重启一次 DSH —— 浏览器半边的模块表在启动时构建；
3. Desktop 版的 profile 由 Electron 独占管理，安装要走应用内「插件 → 添加」。

## 3. 概览显示「未安装」但 REA 明明装过

- 卡片读的是**当前 profile** 的 `node_modules/rea-agents`；多 profile 并存时会各看各的。
- 插件版本与 REA 版本是两个东西：卡片显示的是 REA 的版本。
- 迁移自 dsh-rea-agents 的旧 pin 可以在「维护 → 导入旧 pin」里接过来。

## 4. 引擎显示 `broken`

「文件在，但探测失败」。按序排查：

1. 展开该行「详情」看 `probeOutput`（探测命令的 stderr）；
2. 手动执行同一个命令（例如 `<引擎路径> --version`），确认是不是缺 DLL / 缺 VC 运行库；
3. 重新安装一次（会重下并校验）；
4. 杀软把可执行文件隔离了是最常见原因——加白名单后重装。

## 5. 体检里缺口很多，但卡片说引擎 ok

体检（`GET /doctor`）是**REA 自己的视角**：它按注册的工具反推「需要哪些引擎」，
再报告缺口。两者不一致通常意味着：

- 引擎装好了，但 REA 启动时没拿到环境变量 → 点「热重载 MCP 行」，或重启 DSH；
- 用户自己设的 `REA_*` 环境变量覆盖了插件合成值 → 概览的 `REA_POWERPACK_OVERRIDES`
  会列出被保留的变量名，按需删掉自己的设置。

## 6. 体检结果一直是「旧」的

- `/doctor` 会真实拉起 REA 做一次能力盘点，首次可能要几十秒；
- 卡片上的「最近体检时间」来自 `state.json`，超过检查间隔会标记 stale；
- 点「运行体检」强制刷新，结果同时写回 `state.json`。

## 6.5 doctor 报 `node missing_dependency`（REA 6.8.0 要求 Node >= 24.22.1）

**症状**：能力体检里出现 `node` 缺失、reason `missing_dependency`；但 REA 本身工作正常，
229 个工具都在。

**原因**：REA 6.8.0 的 `rea doctor` 自带一条 Node 版本检查，要求 **Node >= 24.22.1**。
而 Harness 桌面版内嵌的是 Electron 44.7.0 的 Node **24.21.0**，系统 Node 常见为 24.19.0，
两者都低于门槛。这是 doctor 的**自检项**，不是 REA 不能用。

**处理**：

1. 把系统 Node 升到 **24.22.1 或更高**（`node -v` 确认）；
2. 重启 DSH，让 `rea-mcp` 启动器用系统 Node 重新执行自己（启动器会在需要时做这一步）；
3. 再跑一次体检，`node` 这条应从缺口里消失。

只想确认功能是否受影响：随便调一个 REA 工具（如 `binary_session`），能返回工具清单就说明
REA 本身没问题，只是 doctor 的版本自检没过。

## 7. 热重载后工具还是旧的

看返回值里的 `mode`：

- `hot`：已就位；
- `restart-required`：宿主没提供重载钩子（或 `ctx.loader` 不可用），重启 DSH。

另：`cordis.patch.yml` 决定 MCP 行定义。如果你手改过它，热重载会尽量恢复原文件，
失败时以宿主日志里的 `reload_failed` 为准。

## 8. 端口/前缀相关

- 宿主路由只挂在 loopback，前缀固定 `/rea-powerpack`；
- 用绝对路径调用（`fetch('/rea-powerpack/state')`），不要用相对路径；
- 403 + `loopback_only`：你从非本机地址访问了，或被反代到了公网。

## 9. 需要更多线索时

1. 「维护 → 导出诊断报告」，得到一份含状态的 Markdown；
2. 附上 `logs/powerpack.log` 最后 200 行；
3. 附上「能力体检 → 复制报告」的原文；
4. 说明：Windows 版本、Node 版本、DSH 版本、是否用代理、哪些引擎是自己装的。

导出报告与复制报告都只包含路径与版本，不包含被分析文件的内容。

```

`docs/UPDATES.md`:

```md
# 版本、pin 与更新

REA（`rea-agents`）装在 DSH 配置档（profile）里，执行 `pnpm add rea-agents@<版本> --save-exact`。
**dsh-rea-powerpack 从不「跟随 latest」**：它先把你选的版本固定（pin）下来，检查到新版
也按策略决定要不要动，动之前先在影子目录里体检一遍。

对应实现：`lib/pin.js`。

## 1. pin.json 是什么

状态目录（默认 `%LOCALAPPDATA%\rea-powerpack`）下的 `pin.json` 记录「现在固定了什么」：

```json
{
  "version": "6.5.0",
  "requested": "6.5.0",
  "pinnedAt": "2025-08-01T10:00:00.000Z",
  "pinnedFrom": "update",
  "retained": { "from": "6.4.2", "at": "2025-08-01T10:00:00.000Z" },
  "withheldBuilds": ["6.6.0"],
  "latestKnown": "6.6.0",
  "checkedAt": "2025-08-01T09:59:00.000Z"
}
```

| 字段 | 含义 |
| --- | --- |
| `version` | 当前固定并实际装着的版本 |
| `requested` | 你「要求」的版本。与 `version` 不一致时说明安装/体检失败被回滚过 |
| `pinnedFrom` | 谁固定了它：`cli` / `ui` / `update` / `preflight` |
| `retained.from` | 升级前的旧版本，**「回滚」按钮就回滚到它**；没有就说明没有可回滚的目标 |
| `withheldBuilds` | 体检不合格被扣下的版本。这些版本不会被自动重装，直到上游修好 |
| `latestKnown` / `checkedAt` | 最近一次 registry 检查的结果与时间 |

pin.json 缺失或损坏时按「未固定」处理，不会抛异常、不会让卡片崩掉。

## 2. 更新策略

在卡片「REA 版本与更新」里选择，写入 `settings.json` 的 `updatePolicy`：

| 策略 | 检查到新版时 | 适合谁 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `notify`（默认） | 只提示，不升级 | 大多数人的默认：可控、可预期 |
| `auto-idle` | 空闲时自动升级（有正在跑的对话就不动） | 想始终跟进、又不想打断正在进行的分析 |
| `manual` | 什么都不做，只有手动点「升级」才动 | 生产环境、需要严格可复现 |

决策矩阵（实现见 `decideUpgrade`）：

| policy | 有新版？ | 是否空闲 | action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `notify` | 有 | — | `notify` |
| `notify` | 无 | — | `none`（`up_to_date`） |
| `auto-idle` | 有 | 空闲 | `upgrade` |
| `auto-idle` | 有 | 忙 | `notify`（`busy`） |
| `manual` | 有 | — | `none`（`manual_policy`） |
| 任意 | 未安装 REA | — | `none`（`rea_not_installed`） |
| 任意 | 不知道新版 | — | `none`（`no_latest_version_known`） |

「忙」的判定看最近 10 分钟内有没有 API 活动；自动升级不会踩在你正在跑的分析上。

## 3. 一次升级到底做了什么

`upgradeRea(home, profileDir, version)` 是**五步**，任何一步失败都不留半装状态：

1. **影子安装**：在 `<home>/staging/<version>/` 里单独 `pnpm add --save-exact rea-agents@<version>`，
   不碰你正在用的配置档；
2. **影子体检**：用最小 JSON-RPC 客户端拉起影子目录里的 REA
   （`initialize` → `notifications/initialized` → `tools/list` 翻页），
   要求 **serverInfo.version 必须等于目标版本**、**工具数 ≥ 200**；
3. **正式安装**：同样版本写进配置档；
4. **复检**：在配置档里再拉一次，工具数仍须 ≥ 200；
5. **固定**：写 pin.json（`pinnedFrom: 'update'`，`retained` 记下旧版本）。

失败时：

- 第 2 步失败 → 配置档**从没被改过**，只把失败原因报出来；
- 第 4 步失败 → 该版本进 `withheldBuilds`，并**自动回滚**到升级前的版本；
  回滚也失败才算 `rollback_failed`（此时卡片会明说两次失败的原因）。

这也解释了为什么升级会慢：一次真实安装 + 两次完整 REA 握手。超时上限分别是
安装 15 分钟、体检 15 秒。

## 4. 回滚

- 卡片上的「回滚」按钮回到 `pin.retained.from`；没有该字段时按钮禁用并提示原因。
- 回滚同样会做一次握手体检，装不回去就是 `rollback_failed`，配置档保持原样。
- 回滚成功后 `retained` 被清空（旧版本已经被「用掉」），当前版本进 `withheldBuilds`，
  避免自动策略立刻又把你升回那个坏版本。

命令行场景也可以用 `POST /rea-powerpack/update { "rollback": true }`。

## 5. 手动固定到某个版本

在「REA 版本与更新」输入框填精确版本（`6.5.0`，可带 `-rc.1` 这类 prerelease），
点「固定」。校验通过后走与升级相同的五步。

- 已固定到别的版本时再指定新版本会得到 `pin_conflict`——先解除固定，再换版本，
  避免「以为换成功、其实还是旧版」。
- `compareVersions` 按 semver 比较并正确排序 prerelease：`6.8.0-rc.1 < 6.8.0`，
  `6.8.0-rc.1 < 6.8.0-rc.2`，`1.0.0-alpha < 1.0.0-alpha.1`。

## 6. 导入旧 pin

从 dsh-rea-agents 迁移过来时，卡片「维护」区可以填旧 `pin.json` 的路径导入：
版本对得上就直接接管固定状态（`pinnedFrom` 保留，方便追溯），
不会重装 REA。导入只认 `version` 字段合法的文件，坏文件返回 `bad_request`。

## 7. 热重载 vs 重启

升级/回滚后会尝试热重载 MCP 行（`reloadMcpRow`）：

- `mode: 'hot'`：连接已就地重建，无需重启 DSH；
- `mode: 'restart-required'`：宿主没有提供热重载钩子，**必须重启 DSH** 才会用上新版本。
  卡片会把 `reason` 一起显示，不会假装成功。

不光是版本变化：**改引擎路径、改 `toolTimeoutMs` 之后同样建议重载一次**，
因为 REA 的环境变量是在 MCP 子进程启动时合成的。

## 8. 离线与 registry 不可达

检查新版依赖配置档自己的 registry（`pnpm view rea-agents version`，沿用你的
registry / 代理 / 认证配置）。失败会归为：

- `registry_timeout`：registry 没在超时内答应，稍后再试；
- `registry_unreachable`：连不上或被代理挡住。

这两种情况**只影响「检查新版」**：当前 pin 不变、已装的 REA 照常工作。
离线机器不会因此失去 MCP。

pnpm 自己的供应链策略（如 `minimumReleaseAge`）同样生效：新版本发布时间不够长时
pnpm 会拒绝安装，报错原样转成卡片上的错误详情，pin 不会被改动。

## 9. 版本号显示约定

- 路径、版本号一律等宽字体显示，避免 `6.5.0` 和 `6.50` 看起来一样。
- 概览里的「引擎就绪数」是 `ok + external_ok` 的数量，不含 `requires_dependency`。
- 影子预检结果会显示 **serverVersion 与 toolCount** 两项——这是判断「新版到底能不能用」
  最直接的两个数。

```

/**
 * Engine catalog for dsh-rea-powerpack.
 *
 * Pure data + pure query functions. No filesystem, no child processes, no
 * network — every runtime decision lives in engines.js. URLs here are the
 * verified, contract-approved facts; anything that could not be verified is
 * marked `install: null` with a `notes` entry that says so.
 *
 * @see ARCHITECTURE.md §5 for the EngineSpec contract.
 */

/**
 * @typedef {object} EngineInstall
 * @property {'zip'|'file'|'github-latest'|'winget'|'pip'} kind
 * @property {string} [url] Pinned download URL (zip / file).

 * @property {string} [latestUrl] GitHub "latest release" API endpoint.

 * @property {string[]} [requires]

 * @property {EngineInstall|null} install
 * @property {string} [binary] Executable name inside a directory install.

const BY_ID = new Map(ENGINES.map((spec) => [spec.id, spec]))

/**
 * Look one engine up by id.
 * @param {string} id
 * @returns {EngineSpec|undefined}
 */
export function byId(id) {
  if (typeof id !== 'string') return undefined
  return BY_ID.get(id)
}

/**
 * Engines relevant to a platform. Entries whose `platforms` do not include the
 * platform are dropped; entries that exist but are explicitly unsupported on
 * this host (hostUnsupported === platform) are KEPT so the UI can explain them.
 * @param {string} [platform] defaults to process.platform
 * @param {{platform?: string}|undefined} [host]
 * @returns {EngineSpec[]}
 */
export function forPlatform(platform = process.platform, host) {
  const target = typeof platform === 'string' && platform !== '' ? platform : (host?.platform ?? process.platform)
  return ENGINES.filter((spec) => Array.isArray(spec.platforms) && spec.platforms.includes(target))
}

/** Engines that are safe to advertise for installation on this host. */
export function installableEngines(platform = process.platform) {
  return forPlatform(platform).filter((spec) => spec.install !== null && spec.hostUnsupported !== platform)
}

/** Stable list of every environment variable named by the catalog. */
export function envVarNames() {
  const names = new Set(['JAVA_HOME', 'GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR'])
  for (const spec of ENGINES) if (spec.envVar) names.add(spec.envVar)
  return [...names]
}

```

`lib/client.js`:

```js
// Browser half of dsh-rea-powerpack: this bundle's own configuration card.
//
// Hand-written in the lazy-CJS bundle protocol (window.__ModuleLoader__.load
// with a factory returning cordis-plugin exports), so there is no build step
// and the only module it requires is React.
//
// The card is the operations view for REA on Windows: the engine matrix, the
// pinned rea-agents version, the capability doctor and the maintenance
// actions. It renders its own markup and CSS rather than depending on
// product-internal components, so it keeps working across harness versions that
// rearrange those components. The one stable contract it relies on is
// plugins.bundle.config - the plugin page's declared seat for a bundle's own
// configuration, keyed by package name and drawn on that bundle's page.
//
// This half holds no policy of its own. Which engine counts as ready, whether a
// version is newer, how an update is staged and rolled back, and what the pin
// file says are all decided by the host half, which owns the machine-level
// state. The card renders that state, collects the intents a user has here, and
// reports exactly what happened. Every mutation is followed by a fresh GET
// /state so the page shows what is on disk, never a prediction.
window.__ModuleLoader__.load({
  id: 'dsh-rea-powerpack',
  factory: (require) => {
    var module = { exports: {} };
    var exports = module.exports;

    var react = require('react');
    var h = react.createElement;
    var useState = react.useState;
    var useEffect = react.useEffect;
    var useCallback = react.useCallback;

    var API_PREFIX = '/rea-powerpack';

    /**
     * The browser's own language, used whenever the shell cannot answer.
     * Matching is on the primary subtag so zh-Hans and zh-TW both land on
     * Chinese, and anything unrecognised falls through to English.
     */
    function browserLocale() {
      try {
        var tags = [];
        if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined') {
          if (Array.isArray(navigator.languages)) tags = tags.concat(navigator.languages);
          if (typeof navigator.language === 'string') tags.push(navigator.language);
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < tags.length; i += 1) {
          var primary = String(tags[i] || '').slice(0, 2).toLowerCase();
          if (primary === 'zh') return 'zh';
          if (primary === 'en') return 'en';
        }
      } catch (error) {
        // A host without navigator falls through to the shell fallback.
      }
      return 'en';
    }

    /** Narrow a BCP-47 tag to one of the two dictionaries. */
    function toLocaleId(tag) {
      return typeof tag === 'string' && tag.slice(0, 2).toLowerCase() === 'zh' ? 'zh' : 'en';
    }

    /**
     * Build a translate function for one resolved locale. An unknown locale
     * resolves through the browser rather than pinning one language, and a
     * missing key falls back to Chinese and then to the key itself, so a
     * missing sentence is visible instead of rendering "undefined".
     */
    function makeT(locale) {
      var id = locale === 'en' ? 'en' : locale === 'zh' ? 'zh' : browserLocale();
      var table = DICT[id];
      return function t(key, params) {
        var text = table[key] !== undefined ? table[key] : DICT.zh[key] !== undefined ? DICT.zh[key] : key;
        if (params === undefined) return text;
        return String(text).replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, function (match, name) {
          return params[name] !== undefined ? String(params[name]) : match;
        });
      };
    }

    /** Translate for an already-resolved locale id. */
    function translatorFor(localeId) {
      return makeT(toLocaleId(localeId));
    }

    /** How the card decides its language. auto follows the interface. */
    var LANG_MODES = ['auto', 'zh', 'en'];

    /** The remembered manual choice, defaulting to auto. */
    function readLocalePref() {
      try {
        var stored = window.localStorage.getItem(LOCALE_KEY);
        return typeof stored === 'string' && LANG_MODES.indexOf(stored) !== -1 ? stored : 'auto';
      } catch (error) {
        return 'auto';
      }
    }

    /** Remember a choice; a store that refuses only costs persistence. */
    function writeLocalePref(mode) {
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(LOCALE_KEY, mode);
      } catch (error) {
        // The switch still applies for this session.
      }
    }

    /**
     * The shell's current language plus a way to hear about changes. The
     * locale service is bound in apply; until then the browser's language
     * stands in, so the card never pins itself to one language.
     */
    var shellLocale = {
      read: function () {
        return browserLocale();
      },
      subscribe: function () {
        return function () {};
      },
    };

    /**
     * Bind the shell's locale service. The active language lives in the
     * snapshot's active field; reading any other field yields undefined,
     * which compares unequal to zh and would silently pin the card to one
     * language. Centralising the read is what keeps that defect out.
     */
    function bindShellLocale(locale) {
      if (locale === undefined || locale === null) return;
      var listeners = [];
      var live = function () {
        try {
          var snapshot = locale.getLocale();
          var tag = snapshot !== undefined && typeof snapshot.active === 'string' ? snapshot.active : '';
          return tag === '' ? browserLocale() : toLocaleId(tag);
        } catch (error) {
          return browserLocale();
        }
      };
      shellLocale.read = live;
      shellLocale.subscribe = function (listener) {
        listeners.push(listener);
        return function () {
          var at = listeners.indexOf(listener);
          if (at !== -1) listeners.splice(at, 1);
        };
      };
      if (typeof locale.subscribe === 'function') {
        try {
          locale.subscribe(function () {
            var next = live();
            for (var i = 0; i < listeners.length; i += 1) {
              try {
                listeners[i](next);
              } catch (error) {
                if (typeof console !== 'undefined') console.error('[dsh-rea-powerpack] locale listener failed:', error);
              }
            }
          });
        } catch (error) {
          if (typeof console !== 'undefined') console.warn('[dsh-rea-powerpack] locale mirror skipped:', error);
        }
      }
    }

    /** Inject the stylesheet once, keyed by a stable id. */
    function useStyles() {
      useEffect(function () {
        var id = 'dsh-rea-powerpack-styles';
        if (typeof document === 'undefined' || document.getElementById(id) !== null) return;
        var style = document.createElement('style');
        style.id = id;
        style.textContent = CSS;
        document.head.appendChild(style);
      }, []);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- plumbing

    /** An error the host half answered with, carrying its stable error code. */
    function ApiError(code, detail, status) {
      this.name = 'ApiError';
      this.code = code;
      this.detail = detail === undefined ? null : detail;
      this.status = status === undefined ? null : status;
      this.message = code;
    }
    ApiError.prototype = Object.create(Error.prototype);
    ApiError.prototype.constructor = ApiError;

    /**
     * Turn a failed response into an ApiError. The host contract sends
     * {ok:false,error:{code,detail}} for handled failures and {code:'not_found'}
     * for an unknown route, so both shapes are read here; a transport-level
     * failure (non-JSON body, gateway error) still gets a code derived from the
     * status so the card never shows a blank sentence.
     */
    function toApiError(payload, status, text) {
      var code = null;
      var detail = null;
      if (payload && typeof payload === 'object') {
        if (payload.error && typeof payload.error === 'object') {
          if (typeof payload.error.code === 'string') code = payload.error.code;
          if (typeof payload.error.detail === 'string') detail = payload.error.detail;
          else if (typeof payload.error.message === 'string') detail = payload.error.message;
        } else if (typeof payload.code === 'string') {
          code = payload.code;
          if (typeof payload.detail === 'string') detail = payload.detail;
        } else if (typeof payload.reason === 'string') {
          detail = payload.reason;
        }
      }
      if (code === null) code = 'http_' + status;
      if (detail === null && typeof text === 'string' && text !== '' && text.length <= 400) detail = text;
      return new ApiError(code, detail, status);
    }

    /**
     * Read the host's answer as JSON, or as text for the report route. A body
     * that is not JSON is only an error when the status says so: GET /report
     * answers markdown, which is a legitimate 200.
     */
    function apiCall(route, method, body) {
      if (typeof fetch !== 'function') {
        return Promise.reject(new ApiError('bad_request', 'fetch is unavailable in this shell'));
      }
      var init = { method: method, headers: {} };
      if (body !== undefined) {
        init.headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
        init.body = JSON.stringify(body);
      } else if (method === 'POST') {
        init.headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
        init.body = '{}';
      }
      return fetch(API_PREFIX + '/' + route, init).then(function (response) {
        return response.text().then(function (text) {
          var payload = null;
          var parsed = false;
          if (text !== '') {
            try {
              payload = JSON.parse(text);
              parsed = true;
            } catch (error) {
              payload = null;
            }
          }
          if (!response.ok) throw toApiError(parsed ? payload : null, response.status, text);
          if (parsed && payload && payload.error && typeof payload.error === 'object') {
            throw toApiError(payload, response.status, text);
          }
          if (!parsed) return { text: text, status: response.status };
          return payload === null ? {} : payload;
        });
      });
    }

    /**
     * Sentence plus optional detail for a thrown value. ApiError carries a
     * stable code the dictionary owns; anything else is a transport failure,
     * where the raw message is the only useful thing to show.
     */
    function describeError(t, error) {
      if (error && typeof error.code === 'string' && error.code !== '') {
        var key = 'err.' + error.code;
        var translated = t(key);
        return {
          text: translated === key ? t('errGeneric', { code: error.code }) : translated,
          detail: typeof error.detail === 'string' && error.detail !== '' ? error.detail : null,
          code: error.code,
        };
      }
      var reason = error && error.message ? error.message : String(error);
      return { text: t('requestFailedWith', { reason: reason }), detail: null, code: null };
    }

    /** Sentence for a 200 answer that still says ok:false. */
    function payloadFailure(t, payload) {
      var error = payload && payload.error && typeof payload.error === 'object' ? payload.error : null;
      if (error && typeof error.code === 'string') {
        return describeError(t, { code: error.code, detail: typeof error.detail === 'string' ? error.detail : null });
      }
      if (payload && typeof payload.reason === 'string' && payload.reason !== '') {
        return { text: t('actionFailedWith', { reason: payload.reason }), detail: null };
      }
      return { text: t('actionFailedWith', { reason: t('unknown') }), detail: null };
    }

    /** Copy text, preferring the async clipboard and degrading to a selection. */
    function writeClipboard(text) {
      var value = String(text);
      if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.clipboard && typeof navigator.clipboard.writeText === 'function') {
        return navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
      }
      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        try {
          if (typeof document === 'undefined') throw new Error('clipboard is unavailable');
          var area = document.createElement('textarea');
          area.value = value;
          area.setAttribute('readonly', 'readonly');
          area.style.position = 'fixed';
          area.style.opacity = '0';
          document.body.appendChild(area);
          area.select();
          var copied = typeof document.execCommand === 'function' ? document.execCommand('copy') : false;
          document.body.removeChild(area);
          if (copied) resolve();
          else reject(new Error('clipboard is unavailable'));
        } catch (error) {
          reject(error);
        }
      });
    }

    /** Save text as a file through a blob URL; the anchor is removed after use. */
    function downloadText(name, text) {
      try {
        var blob = new Blob([String(text)], { type: 'text/markdown;charset=utf-8' });
        var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        var anchor = document.createElement('a');
        anchor.href = url;
        anchor.download = name;
        anchor.style.display = 'none';
        document.body.appendChild(anchor);
        anchor.click();
        document.body.removeChild(anchor);
        setTimeout(function () {
          URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        }, 0);
      } catch (error) {
        throw new Error(error && error.message ? error.message : 'download is unavailable');
      }
    }

    /** A filename-safe local timestamp. */
    function stamp() {
      var now = new Date();
      var pad = function (value) {
        var text = String(value);
        return text.length < 2 ? '0' + text : text;
      };
      return (
        String(now.getFullYear()) +
        pad(now.getMonth() + 1) +
        pad(now.getDate()) +
        '-' +
        pad(now.getHours()) +
        pad(now.getMinutes()) +
        pad(now.getSeconds())
      );
    }

    /** Contract versions are exact semver - a range would hide which build runs. */
    var VERSION_RE = /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$/;

    function isExactVersion(value) {
      return typeof value === 'string' && VERSION_RE.test(value.trim());
    }

    /** Where the host half keeps machine-level state when settings do not override it. */
    function defaultPowerpackHome(platform) {
      if (platform === 'win32') return '%LOCALAPPDATA%\\rea-powerpack';
      if (platform === 'darwin') return '~/Library/Application Support/rea-powerpack';
      return '$XDG_STATE_HOME/rea-powerpack (default ~/.local/state/rea-powerpack)';
    }

    var STATUS_LABEL_KEYS = {
      ok: 'statusOk',
      missing: 'statusMissing',
      broken: 'statusBroken',
      unsupported_host: 'statusUnsupportedHost',
      requires_dependency: 'statusRequiresDependency',
      external_ok: 'statusExternalOk',
      external_missing: 'statusExternalMissing',
    };

    var STATUS_TONES = {
      ok: 'ok',
      external_ok: 'info',
      missing: 'warn',
      broken: 'err',
      unsupported_host: 'warn',
      requires_dependency: 'warn',
      external_missing: 'muted',
    };

    function statusLabel(t, status) {
      var key = STATUS_LABEL_KEYS[status];
      return key === undefined ? status || t('unknown') : t(key);
    }

    /** The dictionary owns the wording for known reasons; unknown ones stay raw. */
    function reasonLabel(t, reason) {
      if (typeof reason !== 'string' || reason === '') return t('doctorReasonUnknown');
      var key = 'reason.' + reason;
      var translated = t(key);
      return translated === key ? reason : translated;
    }

    /** Policy ids are stable; only their presentation is translated. */
    function policyLabel(t, policy) {
      if (policy === 'auto-idle') return t('policyAutoIdle');
      if (policy === 'manual') return t('policyManual');
      return t('policyNotify');
    }

    /** pinnedFrom is a small enum written by the host half. */
    function pinnedFromLabel(t, source) {
      if (typeof source !== 'string' || source === '') return t('unknown');
      var key = 'pinnedFrom.' + source;
      var translated = t(key);
      return translated === key ? source : translated;
    }

    /**
     * Whether the matrix may offer an install button. The host half sends an
     * explicit installable flag; the fallback only guesses for older hosts,
     * where a plain missing/broken status is the only signal available.
     */
    function engineInstallable(engine) {
      if (engine && typeof engine.installable === 'boolean') return engine.installable;
      return engine !== null && engine !== undefined && (engine.status === 'missing' || engine.status === 'broken');
    }

    /** Managed engines can be removed; probe-only engines cannot. */
    function engineManaged(engine) {
      return engine !== null && engine !== undefined && engine.managed === true;
    }

    /** Ready count over an engine array. */
    function countReady(engines) {
      var ready = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < engines.length; i += 1) {
        if (engines[i] && (engines[i].status === 'ok' || engines[i].status === 'external_ok')) ready += 1;
      }
      return ready;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------- presentation

    /** A labelled fact in a summary grid. */
    function Fact(props) {
      return h(
        'div',
        null,
        h('p', { className: 'rpp-label' }, props.label),
        h('p', { className: 'rpp-value' + (props.mono ? ' rpp-mono' : ''), title: props.title }, props.value),
      );
    }

    /** A status pill. */
    function Badge(props) {
      return h(
        'span',
        {
          className: 'rpp-badge' + (props.tone ? ' rpp-badge-' + props.tone : ''),
          title: props.title,
        },
        props.children,
      );
    }

    /** A titled explanatory note. */
    function Note(props) {
      return h(
        'div',
        { className: 'rpp-note' + (props.warn ? ' rpp-note-warn' : '') },
        props.title === undefined ? null : h('p', { className: 'rpp-note-title' }, props.title),
        h('p', null, props.children),
      );
    }

    /**
     * One button. Busy is expressed on the button itself, so a click always has
     * an immediate visible answer even when the host takes a minute.
     */
    function Btn(props) {
      return h(
        'button',
        {
          type: 'button',
          className: 'rpp-btn' + (props.primary ? ' rpp-btn-primary' : '') + (props.small ? ' rpp-btn-sm' : ''),
          disabled: props.disabled === true,
          title: props.title,
          onClick: props.disabled === true ? undefined : props.onClick,
        },
        props.busy === true ? props.busyLabel : props.label,
      );
    }

    /** Section chrome: heading, optional hint, optional actions, then content. */
    function Panel(props) {
      return h(
        'section',
        { className: 'rpp-section' + (props.first ? ' rpp-section-first' : '') },
        h(
          'div',
          { className: 'rpp-section-head' },
          h(
            'div',
            null,
            h('h3', { className: 'rpp-section-title' }, props.title),
            props.hint === undefined ? null : h('p', { className: 'rpp-section-sub' }, props.hint),
          ),
          props.actions === undefined ? null : h('div', { className: 'rpp-row' }, props.actions),
        ),
        props.children,
      );
    }

    /**
     * Language control. auto follows the interface and is the default; a
     * pinned language overrides it and is remembered across reloads. The two
     * language names stay in their own language by design.
     */
    function LangPicker(props) {
      return h(
        'label',
        { className: 'rpp-lang', title: props.t('langLabel') },
        h('span', null, props.t('langLabel')),
        h(
          'select',
          {
            value: props.pref,
            'aria-label': props.t('langLabel'),
            onChange: function (event) {
              props.onChange(event.target.value);
            },
          },
          h('option', { value: 'auto' }, props.t('langAuto')),
          h('option', { value: 'zh' }, '中文'),
          h('option', { value: 'en' }, 'English'),
        ),
      );
    }

    /** The page header: title, subtitle, language control and a refresh. */
    function renderHeader(t, pref, setPref, onRefresh, busy, loadedAt) {
      return h(
        'div',
        { className: 'rpp-header' },
        h(
          'div',
          null,
          h('h2', { className: 'rpp-title' }, t('pageTitle')),
          h('p', { className: 'rpp-subtitle' }, t('pageSubtitle')),
          loadedAt === null
            ? null
            : h('p', { className: 'rpp-section-sub', style: { marginTop: '2px' } }, t('doctorAt') + ' ' + loadedAt),
        ),
        h(
          'div',
          { className: 'rpp-header-right' },
          h(LangPicker, {
            t: t,
            pref: pref,
            onChange: function (mode) {
              setPref(mode);
            },
          }),
          h(Btn, {
            label: t('refresh'),
            disabled: busy !== null,
            onClick: onRefresh,
          }),
        ),
      );
    }

    /** Pending, success and failure all look different; failure can be expanded. */
    function renderNotice(t, notice) {
      if (notice === null || notice === undefined) return null;
      var tone =
        notice.kind === 'ok' ? 'rpp-notice-ok' : notice.kind === 'warn' ? 'rpp-notice-warn' : 'rpp-notice-err';
      var children = [h('p', { key: 'text' }, notice.text)];
      if (notice.detail !== null && notice.detail !== undefined && notice.detail !== '') {
        if (notice.kind === 'ok') {
          children.push(
            h('pre', { key: 'detail', className: 'rpp-pre', style: { marginTop: '6px' } }, notice.detail),
          );
        } else {
          children.push(
            h(
              'details',
              { key: 'detail', className: 'rpp-details', style: { marginTop: '6px' } },
              h('summary', null, t('details')),
              h('pre', { className: 'rpp-pre' }, notice.detail),
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      return h('div', { className: 'rpp-notice ' + tone, role: 'status', 'aria-live': 'polite' }, children);
    }

    /** Host-level warnings, shown before the sections that would explain them. */
    function renderWarnings(t, model) {
      var items = [];
      if (model.writable === false) {
        items.push(h(Note, { key: 'writable', warn: true }, t('writableWarning')));
      }
      if (model.conflict !== null) {
        items.push(
          h(
            Note,
            { key: 'conflict', warn: true, title: t('conflictTitle') },
            t('conflictBody', { detail: model.conflict.detail || model.conflict.kind || 'rea' }),
          ),
        );
      }
      if (model.mcpPresent === false) {
        items.push(h(Note, { key: 'mcp-missing', warn: true }, t('mcpRowMissing')));
      }
      if (model.mcpDisabled === true) {
        items.push(h(Note, { key: 'mcp-disabled', warn: true }, t('mcpRowDisabled')));
      }
      var warning = model.rea && typeof model.rea.enginesWarning === 'string' ? model.rea.enginesWarning : '';
      if (warning !== '') {
        items.push(h(Note, { key: 'engines-warning', title: t('enginesWarningTitle') }, warning));
      }
      return items;
    }

    /** Section 1: the one-screen answer to "what do I have". */
    function renderOverview(t, model) {
      var facts = [
        h(Fact, { key: 'rea', label: t('reaVersion'), value: model.pinned === null ? t('reaNotInstalled') : model.pinned, mono: model.pinned !== null }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'pin', label: t('pinState'), value: model.installed ? t('pinPinned') : t('pinNotPinned') }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'requested', label: t('pinRequested'), value: model.requested === null ? t('none') : model.requested, mono: true }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'engines', label: t('enginesReady'), value: t('enginesReadyValue', { ready: model.ready, total: model.total }) }),
        h(Fact, {
          key: 'doctor',
          label: t('doctorLast'),
          value: model.doctorInfo.lastAt ? String(model.doctorInfo.lastAt) + (model.doctorInfo.stale === true ? ' · ' + t('doctorStaleShort') : '') : t('never'),
        }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'policy', label: t('updatePolicy'), value: policyLabel(t, model.policy) }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'platform', label: t('hostPlatform'), value: model.host.platform ? String(model.host.platform) : t('unknown'), mono: true }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'node', label: t('hostNode'), value: model.host.node ? String(model.host.node) : t('unknown'), mono: true }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'java', label: t('hostJava'), value: model.host.javaHome ? String(model.host.javaHome) : t('notDetected'), mono: true }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'ghidra', label: t('hostGhidra'), value: model.host.ghidraDir ? String(model.host.ghidraDir) : t('notDetected'), mono: true }),
      ];
      if (model.rea.pinnedAt) {
        facts.push(h(Fact, { key: 'pinnedAt', label: t('pinPinnedAt'), value: String(model.rea.pinnedAt), mono: true }));
      }
      if (model.rea.pinnedFrom) {
        facts.push(h(Fact, { key: 'pinnedFrom', label: t('pinPinnedFrom'), value: pinnedFromLabel(t, model.rea.pinnedFrom) }));
      }
      if (model.rea.source) {
        facts.push(h(Fact, { key: 'source', label: t('pinSource'), value: String(model.rea.source), mono: true }));
      }
      if (model.data.profileDir) {
        facts.push(h(Fact, { key: 'profile', label: t('profileDir'), value: String(model.data.profileDir), mono: true }));
      }
      return h(
        Panel,
        { title: t('secOverview'), first: true },
        h('div', { className: 'rpp-facts' }, facts),
        model.unsupported > 0
          ? h('p', { className: 'rpp-empty' }, t('enginesUnsupportedCount', { count: model.unsupported }))
          : null,
      );
    }

    /** One matrix row, plus an expanded detail row when asked for. */
    function renderEngineRow(t, engine, index, ctx) {
      var id = typeof engine.id === 'string' ? engine.id : 'engine-' + index;
      var status = typeof engine.status === 'string' ? engine.status : 'missing';
      var anyoneBusy = ctx.busy !== null;
      var verifying = ctx.busy === 'verify:' + id;
      var installing = ctx.busy === 'install:' + id;
      var uninstalling = ctx.busy === 'uninstall:' + id;
      var open = ctx.expanded === 'engine:' + id;

      var buttons = [];
      if (status !== 'unsupported_host') {
        buttons.push(
          h(Btn, {
            key: 'verify',
            small: true,
            label: t('engineVerify'),
            busy: verifying,
            busyLabel: t('engineVerifying'),
            disabled: anyoneBusy,
            title: anyoneBusy && !verifying ? t('busyHint') : undefined,
            onClick: function () {
              ctx.onVerify(id);
            },
          }),
        );
      }
      if (engineInstallable(engine)) {
        buttons.push(
          h(Btn, {
            key: 'install',
            small: true,
            primary: true,
            label: status === 'broken' ? t('engineReinstall') : t('engineInstall'),
            busy: installing,
            busyLabel: t('engineInstalling'),
            disabled: anyoneBusy,
            title: anyoneBusy && !installing ? t('busyHint') : undefined,
            onClick: function () {
              ctx.onInstall(id);
            },
          }),
        );
      }
      if (engineManaged(engine)) {
        buttons.push(
          h(Btn, {
            key: 'uninstall',
            small: true,
            label: t('engineUninstall'),
            busy: uninstalling,
            busyLabel: t('engineUninstalling'),
            disabled: anyoneBusy,
            onClick: function () {
              ctx.onUninstall(id, engine.name || id);
            },
          }),
        );
      }
      buttons.push(
        h(Btn, {
          key: 'details',
          small: true,
          label: open ? t('hideDetails') : t('details'),
          disabled: false,
          onClick: function () {
            ctx.onToggle('engine:' + id);
          },
        }),
      );

      var badgeTitle = undefined;
      if (status === 'unsupported_host') badgeTitle = t('engineUnsupportedNoInstall');
      else if (status === 'external_missing' || status === 'external_ok') badgeTitle = t('engineExternalNoInstall');
      else if (status === 'requires_dependency') badgeTitle = t('engineNeedsDependency');

      var rows = [
        h(
          'tr',
          { key: id },
          h(
            'td',
            null,
            h('div', { className: 'rpp-engine-name' }, engine.name ? String(engine.name) : id),
            h('div', { className: 'rpp-engine-id' }, id + (engine.envVar ? ' · ' + engine.envVar : '')),
          ),
          h('td', null, typeof engine.tier === 'number' ? 'T' + engine.tier : t('unknown')),
          h('td', { className: 'rpp-mono' }, engine.version ? String(engine.version) : t('engineVersionUnknown')),
          h('td', null, h(Badge, { tone: STATUS_TONES[status] || 'muted', title: badgeTitle }, statusLabel(t, status))),
          h('td', null, h('div', { className: 'rpp-row' }, buttons)),
        ),
      ];

      if (open) {
        var detailFacts = [];
        if (engine.envVar) detailFacts.push(h(Fact, { key: 'env', label: t('engineEnvVar'), value: String(engine.envVar), mono: true }));
        if (engine.path) detailFacts.push(h(Fact, { key: 'path', label: t('enginePath'), value: String(engine.path), mono: true }));
        if (engine.docs) detailFacts.push(h(Fact, { key: 'docs', label: t('engineDocs'), value: String(engine.docs), mono: true }));
        var detailChildren = [];
        if (detailFacts.length > 0) detailChildren.push(h('div', { key: 'facts', className: 'rpp-facts' }, detailFacts));
        if (engine.remedy) {
          detailChildren.push(
            h('p', { key: 'remedy', className: 'rpp-gap-remedy' }, t('doctorRemedyLine', { remedy: String(engine.remedy) })),
          );
        }
        if (engine.probeOutput) {
          detailChildren.push(
            h(
              'div',
              { key: 'probe' },
              h('p', { className: 'rpp-label' }, t('engineProbeOutput')),
              h('pre', { className: 'rpp-pre' }, String(engine.probeOutput)),
            ),
          );
        }
        if (detailChildren.length === 0) {
          detailChildren.push(h('p', { key: 'empty', className: 'rpp-empty' }, t('none')));
        }
        rows.push(
          h(
            'tr',
            { key: id + '-detail' },
            h('td', { colSpan: 5 }, h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' } }, detailChildren)),
          ),
        );
      }
      return rows;
    }

    /** Section 2: one row per catalog engine, with the actions that make sense. */
    function renderEngines(t, model, actions, busy, expanded, onToggle) {
      var rows = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < model.engines.length; i += 1) {
        var engine = model.engines[i];
        if (engine === null || typeof engine !== 'object') continue;
        var rendered = renderEngineRow(t, engine, i, {
          busy: busy,
          expanded: expanded,
          onVerify: actions.verifyEngine,
          onInstall: actions.installEngine,
          onUninstall: actions.uninstallEngine,
          onToggle: onToggle,
        });
        for (var r = 0; r < rendered.length; r += 1) rows.push(rendered[r]);
      }
      if (model.engines.length === 0) {
        rows.push(h('tr', { key: 'empty' }, h('td', { colSpan: 5, className: 'rpp-empty' }, t('enginesEmpty'))));
      }
      return h(
        Panel,
        {
          title: t('secEngines'),
          hint: t('secEnginesHint'),
          actions: [
            h(Btn, {
              key: 'verify-all',
              label: t('refreshAll'),
              busy: busy === 'verify-all',
              busyLabel: t('engineVerifying'),
              disabled: busy !== null,
              onClick: actions.verifyAll,
            }),
          ],
        },
        h(
          'div',
          { className: 'rpp-table-wrap' },
          h(
            'table',
            { className: 'rpp-table' },
            h(
              'thead',
              null,
              h(
                'tr',
                null,
                h('th', null, t('colName')),
                h('th', null, t('colTier')),
                h('th', null, t('colVersion')),
                h('th', null, t('colStatus')),
                h('th', null, t('colActions')),
              ),
            ),
            h('tbody', null, rows),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    /** Section 3: pinned version, check/upgrade/rollback, policy and the last result. */
    function renderVersion(t, model, actions, busy, pinDraft, setPinDraft, lastResult) {
      var latest = typeof model.rea.latestKnown === 'string' && model.rea.latestKnown !== '' ? model.rea.latestKnown : null;
      var relation = null;
      if (model.pinned !== null && latest !== null) relation = model.pinned === latest ? 0 : 1;
      var facts = [
        h(Fact, { key: 'pinned', label: t('versionPinned'), value: model.pinned === null ? t('reaNotInstalled') : model.pinned, mono: true }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'latest', label: t('versionLatest'), value: latest === null ? t('versionNoCheck') : latest, mono: latest !== null }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'checked', label: t('versionCheckedAt'), value: model.rea.checkedAt ? String(model.rea.checkedAt) : t('never'), mono: true }),
        h(Fact, { key: 'policy', label: t('versionPolicyLabel'), value: policyLabel(t, model.policy) }),
      ];
      var relationBadge =
        relation === null
          ? null
          : relation === 0
            ? h(Badge, { tone: 'ok' }, t('versionUpToDate'))
            : h(Badge, { tone: 'info' }, t('versionBehind'));

      var actionsRow = [
        h(Btn, {
          key: 'check',
          label: t('versionCheck'),
          busy: busy === 'check',
          busyLabel: t('versionChecking'),
          disabled: busy !== null,
          onClick: actions.check,
        }),
        h(Btn, {
          key: 'upgrade',
          primary: true,
          label: latest === null ? t('versionUpgradeAny') : t('versionUpgradeTo', { version: latest }),
          busy: busy === 'update',
          busyLabel: t('versionUpgrading'),
          disabled: busy !== null,
          onClick: function () {
            actions.update(latest === null ? undefined : latest);
          },
        }),
        h(Btn, {
          key: 'rollback',
          label: model.retainedFrom === null ? t('versionRollbackUnavailable') : t('versionRollbackTo', { version: model.retainedFrom }),
          busy: busy === 'rollback',
          busyLabel: t('versionRollingBack'),
          disabled: busy !== null || model.retainedFrom === null,
          title: model.retainedFrom === null ? t('versionRollbackUnavailable') : undefined,
          onClick: function () {
            actions.update(model.retainedFrom);
          },
        }),
      ];

      var pinRow = [
        h('input', {
          key: 'pin-input',
          className: 'rpp-input',
          type: 'text',
          value: pinDraft,
          placeholder: t('versionPinPlaceholder'),
          'aria-label': t('versionPinPlaceholder'),
          onChange: function (event) {
            setPinDraft(event.target.value);
          },
        }),
        h(Btn, {
          key: 'pin',
          label: t('versionPinAction'),
          busy: busy === 'pin',
          busyLabel: t('versionPinning'),
          disabled: busy !== null || pinDraft.trim() === '',
          onClick: actions.pin,
        }),
      ];

      var policySelect = h(
        'div',
        { className: 'rpp-row' },
        h('span', { className: 'rpp-label' }, t('versionPolicyLabel')),
        h(
          'select',
          {
            className: 'rpp-input',
            value: model.policy,
            'aria-label': t('versionPolicyLabel'),
            disabled: busy !== null,
            onChange: function (event) {
              actions.setPolicy(event.target.value);
            },
          },
