```js
    await runPnpm(exec, ['add', '--save-exact', PACKAGE + '@' + target], { cwd: profileDir, timeout: INSTALL_TIMEOUT_MS })
    const installed = await installedVersion(profileDir)
    if (installed !== target) {
      throw coded('install_failed', 'Profile install produced ' + String(installed) + ' instead of ' + target)
    }
    let after
    try {
      const probed = await probeServer(profileDir, { home, timeoutMs: PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS })
      if (probed.toolCount < MIN_TOOL_COUNT) {
        throw coded('preflight_failed', 'Installed build advertised only ' + probed.toolCount + ' tools (need at least ' + MIN_TOOL_COUNT + ')')
      }
      after = { serverVersion: probed.serverInfo && probed.serverInfo.version ? probed.serverInfo.version : null, toolCount: probed.toolCount }
    } catch (error) {
      const withheld = Array.from(new Set([...pin.withheldBuilds, target]))
      await writePin(home, { ...pin, withheldBuilds: withheld })
      if (from) {
        try {
          await rollback(home, profileDir, { exec, log, to: from })
        } catch (rollbackError) {
          throw coded('rollback_failed', 'Upgrade to ' + target + ' failed (' + (error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)) + ') and rollback to ' + from + ' also failed: ' + (rollbackError && rollbackError.message ? rollbackError.message : String(rollbackError)))
        }
      }
      throw coded('preflight_failed', 'Upgrade to ' + target + ' failed and was rolled back to ' + String(from) + ': ' + (error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)))
    }
    const now = new Date().toISOString()
    await writePin(home, {
      version: target,
      requested: target,
      pinnedAt: now,
      pinnedFrom: 'update',
      retained: from && from !== target ? { from, at: now } : null,
      withheldBuilds: pin.withheldBuilds.filter((id) => id !== target),
      latestKnown: pin.latestKnown && compareVersions(pin.latestKnown, target) > 0 ? pin.latestKnown : target,
      checkedAt: now,
    })
    await log('info', 'pin.upgrade', { from, to: target, toolCount: after.toolCount })
    return { ok: true, from, to: target, preflight: before, handshake: after }
  } finally {
    await pruneStaging(home).catch(() => {})
  }
}

/**
 * Roll the profile back to the retained (or explicitly requested) build.
 * @param {string} home
 * @param {string} profileDir
 * @param {{exec?: Function, log?: Function, to?: string}} [options]
 */
export async function rollback(home, profileDir, options = {}) {
  const exec = options.exec || defaultExec
  const log = typeof options.log === 'function' ? options.log : async () => {}
  const pin = await readPin(home)
  const current = await installedVersion(profileDir)
  const target = options.to || (pin.retained && pin.retained.from) || pin.version
  if (!target) throw coded('rollback_failed', 'No retained version to roll back to.')
  if (!parseVersion(target)) throw coded('rollback_failed', 'Retained version is not a valid semver: ' + target)
  await runPnpm(exec, ['add', '--save-exact', PACKAGE + '@' + target], { cwd: profileDir, timeout: INSTALL_TIMEOUT_MS })
  const installed = await installedVersion(profileDir)
  if (installed !== target) throw coded('rollback_failed', 'Rollback installed ' + String(installed) + ' instead of ' + target)
  const probed = await probeServer(profileDir, { home, timeoutMs: PREFLIGHT_TIMEOUT_MS })
  if (probed.toolCount < MIN_TOOL_COUNT) {
    throw coded('rollback_failed', 'Rolled back build advertised only ' + probed.toolCount + ' tools.')
  }
  const now = new Date().toISOString()
  await writePin(home, {
    version: target,
    requested: target,
    pinnedAt: now,
    pinnedFrom: 'update',
    retained: null,
    withheldBuilds: Array.from(new Set([...pin.withheldBuilds, ...(current && current !== target ? [current] : [])])),
    latestKnown: pin.latestKnown,
    checkedAt: now,
  })
  await log('info', 'pin.rollback', { from: current, to: target })
  return { ok: true, from: current, to: target, toolCount: probed.toolCount }
}

```

`lib/rea-mcp.mjs`:

```mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * MCP launcher for dsh-rea-powerpack.
 *
 * The host MCP client row starts this file as
 *   node lib/rea-mcp.mjs <profileDir> mcp
 * and this file replaces its own process image with the pinned REA entry point
 * after composing the engine environment. It stays in the same process on
 * purpose: MCP needs single ownership of stdin/stdout.
 *
 * When the current Node is older than the range rea-agents declares in its
 * package.json engines.node, the launcher re-executes itself with a suitable
 * Node found on PATH so REA's own doctor stops reporting node as missing.
 * The re-executed child inherits stdio, so the JSON-RPC pipe stays intact.
 *
 * @see ARCHITECTURE.md §5 (lib/rea-mcp.mjs)
 */
import { createRequire } from 'node:module'
import { execFileSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process'
import { existsSync, readFileSync, realpathSync, statSync } from 'node:fs'
import { delimiter, join, resolve } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'

import { composeEnv, powerpackHome, readSettings } from './engines.js'

const TRACE = process.env.REA_POWERPACK_TRACE === '1'
const REEXEC_ENV = 'REA_POWERPACK_EXEC'
const SELF_PATH = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)
/** Used only when rea-agents/package.json cannot be read. */
const REA_MIN_NODE = '24.22.1'

/** Write one diagnostic line to stderr (stdout belongs to MCP). */
function warn(message) {
  process.stderr.write('rea-powerpack: ' + message + '\n')
}

/** Thrown by fail() so the top-level handler can flush stderr before exiting. */
class LauncherError extends Error {}

function fail(message) {
  // Do not call process.exit() here: on Windows the stderr pipe is written
  // asynchronously, so exiting immediately silently drops the message.
  throw new LauncherError(message)
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------- versions --

/** Parse a dotted version (1-3 components), or null when none is present. */
export function parseVersion(text) {
  const match = /(\d+)(?:\.(\d+))?(?:\.(\d+))?/.exec(String(text === undefined || text === null ? '' : text))
  if (!match) return null
  const major = Number(match[1])
  const minor = Number(match[2] || 0)
  const patch = Number(match[3] || 0)
  return { major: major, minor: minor, patch: patch, text: major + '.' + minor + '.' + patch }
}

/** Compare two version strings; returns -1, 0 or 1. */
export function compareVersions(left, right) {
  const a = parseVersion(left)
  const b = parseVersion(right)
  if (!a && !b) return 0
  if (!a) return -1
  if (!b) return 1
  if (a.major !== b.major) return a.major < b.major ? -1 : 1
  if (a.minor !== b.minor) return a.minor < b.minor ? -1 : 1
  if (a.patch !== b.patch) return a.patch < b.patch ? -1 : 1
  return 0
}

function matchComparator(version, token) {
  if (token === '' || token === '*' || token === 'x' || token === 'X') return true
  const parsed = /^(>=|<=|>|<|=|\^|~)?(.*)$/.exec(token)
  const operator = parsed ? parsed[1] || '' : ''
  const body = parsed ? parsed[2] : token
  const target = parseVersion(body)
  if (!target) return false
  const sign = compareVersions(version.text, target.text)
  switch (operator) {
    case '>':
      return sign > 0
    case '>=':
      return sign >= 0
    case '<':
      return sign < 0
    case '<=':
      return sign <= 0
    case '^':
      if (sign < 0) return false
      if (target.major > 0) return version.major === target.major
      if (target.minor > 0) return version.major === 0 && version.minor === target.minor
      return version.major === 0 && version.minor === 0 && version.patch === target.patch
    case '~':
      return sign >= 0 && version.major === target.major && version.minor === target.minor
    default:
      return sign === 0
  }
}

/** Does a version satisfy a semver range such as "^22.23.4 || ^24.22.1 || >=26.12.1"? */
export function satisfiesRange(version, range) {
  const current = parseVersion(version)
  if (!current) return false
  const clauses = String(range === undefined || range === null ? '' : range)
    .split('||')
    .map((clause) => clause.trim())
    .filter((clause) => clause !== '')
  if (clauses.length === 0) return true
  return clauses.some((clause) =>
    clause
      .split(/\s+/)
      .filter((token) => token !== '')
      .every((token) => matchComparator(current, token)),
  )
}

/** Lowest version mentioned by a range, for user-facing messages. */
export function minimumRequired(range) {
  const tokens = String(range === undefined || range === null ? '' : range)
    .split('||')
    .join(' ')
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter((token) => token !== '')
  let lowest = null
  for (const token of tokens) {
    const parsed = parseVersion(token)
    if (!parsed) continue
    if (lowest === null || compareVersions(parsed.text, lowest) < 0) lowest = parsed.text
  }
  return lowest
}

/**
 * Pick the first candidate whose Node version satisfies the requirement.
 * Candidates are { path, version } pairs; the return value is the candidate
 * itself or null. Pure so the selection policy is unit-testable.
 */
export function selectNode(candidates, required) {
  if (!Array.isArray(candidates)) return null
  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== 'object') continue
    const version = candidate.version || candidate.text || null
    if (!version) continue
    if (satisfiesRange(version, required)) return candidate
  }
  return null
}

// ------------------------------------------------------- node re-exec --

/** Read REA's own engines.node requirement; fall back to REA_MIN_NODE. */
export function readReaNodeRange(profileDir) {
  try {
    const require = createRequire(join(profileDir, 'package.json'))
    const manifestPath = require.resolve('rea-agents/package.json')
    const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, 'utf8'))
    const range = manifest && manifest.engines ? manifest.engines.node : null
    if (typeof range === 'string' && range.trim() !== '') return range.trim()
  } catch {
    // fall through to the built-in floor
  }
  return '>=' + REA_MIN_NODE
}

function nodeVersionOf(execPath) {
  try {
    const output = execFileSync(execPath, ['-v'], {
      encoding: 'utf8',
      timeout: 5000,
      windowsHide: true,
      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'ignore'],
    })
    const parsed = parseVersion(output)
    return parsed ? parsed.text : null
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

function pathNodeNames() {
  if (process.platform === 'win32') return ['node.exe', 'node.cmd', 'node.bat', 'node']
  return ['node']
}

/**
 * Candidate Node executables: REA_POWERPACK_NODE first, then PATH order.
 * process.execPath is excluded on purpose - under the desktop host it is the
 * Electron binary (or a Node older than REA requires), never the fix.
 */
export function collectNodeCandidates(env, selfPath, execPath) {
  const environment = env && typeof env === 'object' ? env : {}
  const self = String(selfPath || '').toLowerCase()
  const own = String(execPath || '').toLowerCase()
  const list = []
  const seen = new Set()
  const push = (candidatePath) => {
    if (typeof candidatePath !== 'string' || candidatePath.trim() === '') return
    let absolute
    try {
      absolute = resolve(candidatePath.trim())
    } catch {
      return
    }
    const key = absolute.toLowerCase()
    if (key === self || key === own || seen.has(key)) return
    seen.add(key)
    try {
      if (!existsSync(absolute) || !statSync(absolute).isFile()) return
    } catch {
      return
    }
    const version = nodeVersionOf(absolute)
    if (!version) return
    list.push({ path: absolute, version: version })
  }
  push(environment.REA_POWERPACK_NODE)
  const pathValue = environment.PATH || environment.Path || ''
  const dirs = String(pathValue).split(delimiter).filter((dir) => dir !== '')
  const names = pathNodeNames()
  for (const dir of dirs) {
    for (const name of names) push(join(dir, name))
  }
  return list
}

/**
 * Decide how the launcher should proceed. Pure so the policy can be unit
 * tested without spawning anything:
 *   { action: 'continue', reason }  stay in this process
 *   { action: 'run', candidate }    hand over to candidate.path
 */
export function planReexec(environment, currentVersion, range, candidates) {
  const env = environment && typeof environment === 'object' ? environment : {}
  if (env[REEXEC_ENV] === '1') return { action: 'continue', reason: 'already-reexecuted' }
  const current = parseVersion(currentVersion)
  if (current && satisfiesRange(current.text, range)) return { action: 'continue', reason: 'satisfied' }
  const chosen = selectNode(candidates, range)
  if (!chosen) {
    return {
      action: 'continue',
      reason: 'no-candidate',
      warn: 'current node ' + currentVersion + ' is below REA requirement ' + range + '; continuing anyway',
    }
  }
  return { action: 'run', candidate: chosen }
}

/**
 * Re-execute this launcher with a Node that satisfies REA's requirement.
 * Returns false when no re-exec is needed or possible; otherwise it hands the
 * process over to the child and never returns.
 */
async function maybeReexec(profileDir) {
  const range = readReaNodeRange(profileDir)
  const candidates = collectNodeCandidates(process.env, SELF_PATH, process.execPath)
  const plan = planReexec(process.env, process.version, range, candidates)
  if (plan.action === 'continue') {
    if (plan.warn) warn(plan.warn)
    return false
  }
  const chosen = plan.candidate

  warn('node ' + process.version + ' is below REA requirement ' + range + '; re-executing with ' + chosen.path + ' (' + chosen.version + ')')
  const argv = process.argv.slice(1)
  const environment = { ...process.env }
  environment[REEXEC_ENV] = '1'
  await new Promise((resolvePromise) => {
    const child = spawn(chosen.path, [SELF_PATH, ...argv.slice(1)], {
      stdio: 'inherit',
      env: environment,
      windowsHide: true,
    })
    child.on('error', (error) => {
      warn('re-exec with ' + chosen.path + ' failed (' + (error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)) + '); continuing on ' + process.version)
      resolvePromise(false)
    })
    child.on('exit', (code, signal) => {
      process.exit(code === null || code === undefined ? (signal ? 1 : 0) : code)
    })
  })
  return false
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------ main flow --

function resolveReaEntry(profileDir) {
  const require = createRequire(join(profileDir, 'package.json'))
  let entry = null
  try {
    entry = require.resolve('rea-agents/scripts/rea.mjs')
  } catch {
    entry = null
  }
  if (!entry) {
    fail(
      'rea-agents is not installed in ' + profileDir +
      '; open Settings -> REA Powerpack -> Install',
    )
  }
  return entry
}

/**
 * Compose the engine environment, but never block startup on it: a broken
 * engines.json must only degrade individual REA tools, not the MCP server.
 */
async function applyEngineEnv(home) {
  try {
    const settings = await readSettings(home)
    const composed = await composeEnv(home, settings)
    if (composed && composed.env && typeof composed.env === 'object') {
      for (const key of Object.keys(composed.env)) {
        const value = composed.env[key]
        if (typeof value !== 'string') continue
        if (key === 'REA_POWERPACK_OVERRIDES') {
          process.env.REA_POWERPACK_OVERRIDES = value
          continue
        }
        process.env[key] = value
      }
    }
    if (composed && Array.isArray(composed.pathPrepend) && composed.pathPrepend.length > 0) {
      const extra = composed.pathPrepend.filter((dir) => typeof dir === 'string' && dir !== '')
      if (extra.length > 0) {
        process.env.PATH = extra.join(delimiter) + delimiter + (process.env.PATH || '')
      }
    }
    if (composed && Array.isArray(composed.missing) && composed.missing.length > 0) {
      warn(
        'engine(s) not installed: ' + composed.missing.join(', ') +
        ' (matching REA tools will report provider_unavailable)',
      )
    }
    if (composed && Array.isArray(composed.warnings)) {
      for (const line of composed.warnings) warn(line)
    }
    if (TRACE) {
      const keys = Object.keys(process.env)
        .filter((key) => key.startsWith('REA_') || key === 'JAVA_HOME' || key === 'GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR')
        .sort()
      warn('composed env keys: ' + keys.join(', '))
    }
  } catch (error) {
    warn(
      'engine environment unavailable (' +
      (error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)) +
      '); continuing without it',
    )
  }
}

/**
 * Compare two paths as the same file, tolerating symlinks/junctions.
 *
 * The host launches this file through the profile's `node_modules` entry, which
 * is a junction to the unpacked plugin directory, while `import.meta.url` has
 * already been resolved to the real path by Node's ESM loader. Comparing the
 * raw strings therefore fails and the launcher would silently do nothing.
 */
function samePath(a, b) {
  const left = resolve(a)
  const right = resolve(b)
  if (left.toLowerCase() === right.toLowerCase()) return true
  try {
    // realpathSync echoes the caller's casing for an already-real path, so the
    // two results must be compared case-insensitively on Windows.
    return realpathSync(left).toLowerCase() === realpathSync(right).toLowerCase()
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

/** True only when this file is the process entry point, not an import. */
export function isEntryPoint(argv, selfPath) {
  const entry = Array.isArray(argv) ? argv[1] : null
  if (typeof entry !== 'string' || entry === '') return false
  if (typeof selfPath !== 'string' || selfPath === '') return false
  try {
    return samePath(entry, selfPath)
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

async function main() {
  const argv = process.argv.slice(2)
  const profileDir = argv[0]
  if (!profileDir || profileDir === '') {
    fail('missing profile directory; usage: rea-mcp.mjs <profileDir> mcp')
  }

  // Take over with a suitable Node first: the host starts this file with
  // process.execPath, which may be an Electron runtime older than REA wants.
  await maybeReexec(profileDir)

  const entry = resolveReaEntry(profileDir)
  const home = powerpackHome(profileDir)
  await applyEngineEnv(home)

  // Hand stdio to REA in this same process.
  process.argv = [process.argv[0], entry, ...argv.slice(1)]
  await import(pathToFileURL(entry).href)
}

if (isEntryPoint(process.argv, SELF_PATH)) {
  try {
    await main()
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof LauncherError)) throw error
    warn(error.message)
    process.exitCode = 1
  }
}

```

`lib/reload.js`:

```js
/**
 * Restart-free reload of the MCP row this plugin installed.
 *
 * The row lives in the host's loader, not in this plugin's fiber, so the only
 * supported way to restart it is disable -> enable on the loader entry. The
 * cordis patch file is snapshotted first: when the host rewrites it during the
 * reload (normalisation, a concurrent plugin install) the snapshot is written
 * back so the row definition never drifts.
 *
 * @see ARCHITECTURE.md §5 (lib/reload.js)
 */
import { readFile, rename, stat, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { homedir } from 'node:os'

import { coded } from './engines.js'

/** How long we wait for the loader to rebuild the row's fiber. */
export const RELOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 15 * 1000

/** Resolve the DSH profile directory the same way the host does. */
export function profileDir(ctx) {
  const fromContext = ctx && ctx.profileContext && typeof ctx.profileContext.dir === 'string' ? ctx.profileContext.dir : null
  if (fromContext && fromContext !== '') return fromContext
  const fromEnv = process.env.DSH_PROFILE_DIR
  if (typeof fromEnv === 'string' && fromEnv !== '') return fromEnv
  const home = process.env.DSH_HOME && process.env.DSH_HOME !== '' ? process.env.DSH_HOME : join(homedir(), '.dsh')
  return join(home, 'profiles', process.env.DSH_PROFILE || 'desktop')
}

function patchFileFor(ctx, opts) {
  return (opts && opts.patchFile) || join(profileDir(ctx), 'cordis.patch.yml')
}

/** Read the patch file as exact bytes plus its mtime. */
async function snapshotPatch(file) {
  try {
    const [bytes, info] = await Promise.all([readFile(file), stat(file)])
    return { file, bytes, mtimeMs: info.mtimeMs }
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

/** Write the snapshot back when the file changed; returns whether we did. */
async function restorePatch(snapshot) {
  if (!snapshot) return false
  let current = null
  try {
    current = await readFile(snapshot.file)
  } catch {
    current = null
  }
  if (current && Buffer.compare(current, snapshot.bytes) === 0) return false
  const temporary = snapshot.file + '.' + process.pid + '.restore'
  await writeFile(temporary, snapshot.bytes)
  await rename(temporary, snapshot.file)
  return true
}

function findRow(loader, rowId) {
  if (!loader || typeof loader.entries !== 'function') return null
  const wanted = String(rowId)
  for (const entry of loader.entries()) {
    if (!entry) continue
    if (entry.id === wanted) return entry
    if (entry.options && entry.options.id === wanted) return entry
    const name = entry.options && entry.options.name
    if (typeof name === 'string' && name === wanted) return entry
  }
  return null
}

async function setDisabled(loader, entry, disabled) {
  if (loader && typeof loader.update === 'function') {
    await loader.update(entry.id, { disabled })
    return
  }
  if (entry && typeof entry.update === 'function') {
    await entry.update({ disabled })
    return
  }
  throw coded('reload_failed', 'The host loader does not expose an update API.')
}

/**
 * Restart one loader row in place.
 * @param {object} ctx host plugin context (must expose ctx.loader)
 * @param {string} rowId loader entry id, e.g. 'rea-powerpack-mcp'
 * @param {{patchFile?: string, timeoutMs?: number}} [opts]
 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, mode: 'hot'|'restart-required', reason?: string, restoredPatch: boolean}>}
 */
export async function reloadMcpRow(ctx, rowId, opts = {}) {
  const timeoutMs = opts.timeoutMs || RELOAD_TIMEOUT_MS
  const loader = ctx && ctx.loader
  const snapshot = await snapshotPatch(patchFileFor(ctx, opts))
  if (!loader || typeof loader.entries !== 'function') {
    const restoredPatch = await restorePatch(snapshot).catch(() => false)
    return { ok: false, mode: 'restart-required', reason: 'The host loader is unavailable (restart DeepSeek Harness).', restoredPatch }
  }
  const row = findRow(loader, rowId)
  if (!row) {
    const restoredPatch = await restorePatch(snapshot).catch(() => false)
    return { ok: false, mode: 'restart-required', reason: 'Loader row ' + rowId + ' is not present.', restoredPatch }
  }
  const beforeFiber = row.fiber || null
  const seen = new Set()
  let rebuilt = false
  let watcher = null
  try {
    if (ctx && typeof ctx.on === 'function') {
      watcher = ctx.on('internal/plugin', (fiber) => {
        if (!fiber) return
        if (fiber === beforeFiber) return
        if (fiber.entry === row || (row.fiber && fiber === row.fiber)) {
          seen.add(fiber.uid)
          if (row.fiber && row.fiber === fiber && row.disabled === false) rebuilt = true
        }
      })
    }
  } catch {
    watcher = null
  }
  try {
    await setDisabled(loader, row, true)
    await setDisabled(loader, row, false)
    const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs
    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
      if (row.disabled === false && row.fiber && row.fiber !== beforeFiber) {
        rebuilt = true
        break
      }
      if (typeof loader.await === 'function') {
        await Promise.race([loader.await(), new Promise((resolvePromise) => setTimeout(resolvePromise, 100))])
      } else {
        await new Promise((resolvePromise) => setTimeout(resolvePromise, 100))
      }
    }
    if (typeof loader.await === 'function') {
      await Promise.race([loader.await(), new Promise((resolvePromise) => setTimeout(resolvePromise, 1000))]).catch(() => {})
    }
    const restoredPatch = await restorePatch(snapshot).catch(() => false)
    if (rebuilt || (row.disabled === false && row.fiber)) {
      return { ok: true, mode: 'hot', restoredPatch }
    }
    return { ok: false, mode: 'restart-required', reason: 'The row did not rebuild within ' + Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000) + 's.', restoredPatch }
  } catch (error) {
    const restoredPatch = await restorePatch(snapshot).catch(() => false)
    const message = error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)
    return { ok: false, mode: 'restart-required', reason: message, restoredPatch }
  } finally {
    if (typeof watcher === 'function') {
      try {
        watcher()
      } catch {
        /* already disposed with the fiber */
      }
    }
  }
}

```

`lib/version.js`:

```js
/**
 * Single source of truth for the plugin's own identity.
 *
 * The published package.json is the only version number in the repository:
 * /state, the engine download User-Agent and the MCP handshake clientInfo all
 * read it from here so they cannot drift after a release bump.
 */
import { createRequire } from 'node:module'

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url)
const manifest = require('../package.json')

export const PACKAGE_NAME = manifest.name
export const PACKAGE_VERSION = manifest.version

```

`package.json`:

```json
{
  "name": "dsh-rea-powerpack",
  "version": "0.1.3",
  "description": "Maxed-out REA for DeepSeek Harness: managed analysis engines (adb, apktool, jadx, Ghidra, WABT, frida), a pinned rea-agents MCP server, health-checked updates and a capability doctor.",
  "license": "MIT",
  "keywords": [
    "deepseek-harness",
    "dsh",
    "dsh-plugin",
```

`test/catalog.test.mjs`:

```mjs
// Catalog integrity. The catalog is pure data, so every rule in contract
// section 5 can be checked without touching the machine.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  CATALOG_VERSION,
  ENGINES,
  byId,
  forPlatform,
  installableEngines,
  envVarNames,
} from '../lib/catalog.js';

const INSTALL_KINDS = ['zip', 'file', 'github-latest', 'winget', 'pip'];
const TIERS = [0, 1, 2];
const ID_PATTERN = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/;
const ENV_PATTERN = /^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*$/;
const SHA_PATTERN = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/;

test('catalog: version is 1 and there are 21 engines', function () {
  assert.equal(CATALOG_VERSION, 1);
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(ENGINES));
  assert.equal(ENGINES.length, 21, 'twenty-one engines: 2 tier-0, 6 tier-1, 13 tier-2');
});

test('catalog: ids are unique, lowercase and stable', function () {
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    assert.equal(typeof spec.id, 'string', 'every engine has an id');
    assert.match(spec.id, ID_PATTERN, 'id is kebab-case: ' + spec.id);
    assert.ok(!seen.has(spec.id), 'duplicate id: ' + spec.id);
    seen.add(spec.id);
  }
  assert.equal(seen.size, ENGINES.length);
});

test('catalog: ids cover the contract roster', function () {
  const expected = [
    'java', 'ghidra',
    'adb', 'apktool', 'jadx', 'wabt', 'frida', 'python',
    'objdump', 'rizin', 'dotnet', 'ilspy', 'mitmproxy',
    'tshark', 'capinfos', 'binwalk', 'unblob', 'sigrok', 'pwntools', 'wakaru', 'process-capture',
  ];
  for (const id of expected) assert.ok(byId(id) !== undefined, 'catalog has ' + id);
});

test('catalog: tier is 0, 1 or 2 on every entry', function () {
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    assert.ok(TIERS.includes(spec.tier), 'tier of ' + spec.id + ' is valid: ' + spec.tier);
  }
  const tierCounts = [0, 1, 2].map(function count(tier) {
    return ENGINES.filter(function same(spec) {
      return spec.tier === tier;
    }).length;
  });
  assert.deepEqual(tierCounts, [2, 6, 13]);
});

test('catalog: names are human-readable and unique', function () {
  const names = new Set();
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    assert.equal(typeof spec.name, 'string', spec.id + ' has a name');
    assert.ok(spec.name.length > 1, spec.id + ' name is not empty');
    assert.ok(!names.has(spec.name), 'duplicate name: ' + spec.name);
    names.add(spec.name);
  }
});

test('catalog: envVar follows SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE and envKind fits it', function () {
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    if (spec.envVar === null) {
      assert.equal(spec.envKind, null, spec.id + ' has no envVar so envKind is null');
      continue;
    }
    assert.match(spec.envVar, ENV_PATTERN, spec.id + ' envVar: ' + spec.envVar);
    assert.ok(['file', 'dir'].includes(spec.envKind), spec.id + ' envKind is file or dir');
    assert.equal(typeof spec.pathPrepend, 'boolean', spec.id + ' declares pathPrepend');
  }
});

test('catalog: tier 0 names an envVar; tier 1 reaches the MCP through one', function () {
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    if (spec.tier === 0) assert.notEqual(spec.envVar, null, spec.id + ' (tier 0) has an envVar');
    if (spec.tier === 1) {
      assert.ok(
        spec.envVar !== null || spec.pathPrepend === true,
        spec.id + ' (tier 1) is exported via envVar or PATH',
      );
    }
  }
});

test('catalog: every declared sha256 is 64 lowercase hex characters', function () {
  let checked = 0;
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    const digests = [];
    if (spec.install && typeof spec.install.sha256 === 'string') digests.push(spec.install.sha256);
    if (typeof spec.sha256 === 'string') digests.push(spec.sha256);
    for (const digest of digests) {
      assert.match(digest, SHA_PATTERN, spec.id + ' sha256 is 64 hex chars');
      checked += 1;
    }
  }
  assert.ok(checked >= 1, 'at least the pinned jadx jar carries a digest');
  assert.equal(
    byId('jadx').install.sha256,
    '6e5eacf500b64292bfb73c49797c1958f6ee44646e43e868039ae7feb573ff75',
    'the jadx jar digest is the audited one',
  );
});

test('catalog: install.kind is one of the five supported kinds', function () {
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    if (spec.install === null) continue;
    assert.ok(INSTALL_KINDS.includes(spec.install.kind), spec.id + ' install kind: ' + spec.install.kind);
    if (spec.install.kind === 'zip' || spec.install.kind === 'file') {
      assert.ok(
        typeof spec.install.url === 'string' || typeof spec.install.urlTemplate === 'string',
        spec.id + ' download kind carries a url or urlTemplate',
      );
    }
    if (spec.install.kind === 'github-latest') {
      assert.equal(typeof spec.install.latestUrl, 'string', spec.id + ' latestUrl');
      assert.equal(typeof spec.install.asset, 'string', spec.id + ' asset template');
    }
    if (spec.install.kind === 'winget') {
      assert.match(spec.install.wingetId, /^[A-Za-z0-9.]+$/, spec.id + ' wingetId');
    }
    if (spec.install.kind === 'pip') {
      assert.ok(Array.isArray(spec.install.pipArgs) && spec.install.pipArgs.length > 0, spec.id + ' pipArgs');
    }
  }
});

test('catalog: engines offered for installation are installable on win32', function () {
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    if (spec.install === null) continue;
    assert.ok(spec.platforms.includes('win32'), spec.id + ' installable entries list win32');
    assert.notEqual(spec.hostUnsupported, 'win32', spec.id + ' is not both installable and unsupported');
  }
});

test('catalog: win32-unsupported engines are kept for the UI and explain themselves', function () {
  const unsupported = ENGINES.filter(function filter(spec) {
    return spec.hostUnsupported === 'win32';
  });
  assert.equal(unsupported.length, 8, 'eight Linux-only lanes are advertised as unsupported');
  for (const spec of unsupported) {
    assert.equal(typeof spec.notes, 'string', spec.id + ' has notes');
    assert.ok(spec.notes.length > 20, spec.id + ' notes explain the limitation');
    assert.equal(spec.install, null, spec.id + ' is not installable');
    assert.ok(spec.platforms.includes('win32'), spec.id + ' stays visible on win32');
  }
  const ids = unsupported.map(function id(spec) {
    return spec.id;
  });
  assert.deepEqual(ids, ['tshark', 'capinfos', 'binwalk', 'unblob', 'sigrok', 'pwntools', 'wakaru', 'process-capture']);
});

test('catalog: probe shape is executable, args array, optional match', function () {
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    if (spec.probe === null) continue;
    assert.equal(typeof spec.probe.exe, 'string', spec.id + ' probe exe');
    assert.ok(spec.probe.exe.length > 0);
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(spec.probe.args), spec.id + ' probe args is an array');
    for (const arg of spec.probe.args) assert.equal(typeof arg, 'string', spec.id + ' probe arg is a string');
    if (spec.probe.match !== undefined) assert.equal(typeof spec.probe.match, 'string', spec.id + ' probe match');
  }
});

test('catalog: requires names only engines that exist', function () {
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    for (const dependency of spec.requires || []) {
      assert.ok(byId(dependency) !== undefined, spec.id + ' requires known engine ' + dependency);
      assert.notEqual(dependency, spec.id, spec.id + ' does not require itself');
    }
  }
});

test('catalog: forPlatform keeps unsupported entries so the UI can explain them', function () {
  const win = forPlatform('win32');
  assert.equal(win.length, ENGINES.length, 'every catalog entry applies to win32');
  assert.deepEqual(forPlatform('linux'), [], 'no catalog entry claims Linux');
  assert.deepEqual(forPlatform('darwin'), [], 'no catalog entry claims macOS');
  // The catalog is Windows-targeted, so on a non-Windows host the default is
  // legitimately empty: assert the default tracks the host instead of assuming it.
  assert.deepEqual(forPlatform(), forPlatform(process.platform), 'defaulting to the host keeps entries');
});

test('catalog: installableEngines excludes the unsupported lanes', function () {
  const installable = installableEngines('win32');
  const ids = installable.map(function id(spec) {
    return spec.id;
  });
  assert.deepEqual(ids, ['adb', 'apktool', 'jadx', 'wabt', 'python', 'frida', 'dotnet', 'mitmproxy']);
  for (const spec of installable) assert.equal(spec.hostUnsupported, undefined);
});

test('catalog: byId answers undefined for junk instead of throwing', function () {
  assert.equal(byId('nope'), undefined);
  assert.equal(byId(''), undefined);
  assert.equal(byId(null), undefined);
  assert.equal(byId(undefined), undefined);
  assert.equal(byId(42), undefined);
  assert.equal(byId('jadx').id, 'jadx');
});

test('catalog: envVarNames is stable, unique and includes the external pair', function () {
  const names = envVarNames();
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(names));
  assert.equal(new Set(names).size, names.length, 'no duplicates');
  assert.ok(names.includes('JAVA_HOME'));
  assert.ok(names.includes('GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR'));
  assert.ok(names.includes('REA_ADB_PATH'));
  assert.ok(!names.includes('REA_POWERPACK_HOME'), 'the powerpack home is not an engine variable');
});

```

`test/client-bundle.mjs`:

```mjs
// Loads lib/client.js the way the harness does: a fake window.__ModuleLoader__
// records the bundle's factory, a minimal React stub lets the card render once,
// and a fake host answers GET /state so the summary path exercises real data.
// Everything here stays in-process: no network, no DOM, no disk writes.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import vm from 'node:vm';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const bundlePath = path.join(here, '..', 'lib', 'client.js');

/** A React stub: element records, plus the three hooks the card uses. */
function makeReact() {
  const store = new Map();
  let current = null;
  let cursor = 0;

  function createElement(type, props) {
    const resolved = Object.assign({}, props === null || props === undefined ? {} : props);
    const children = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 2);
    if (children.length === 1) resolved.children = children[0];
    else if (children.length > 1) resolved.children = children;
    return { vnode: true, type: type, props: resolved };
  }

  function useState(initial) {
    const holder = current;
    const index = cursor;
    cursor += 1;
    if (holder.values[index] === undefined) {
      holder.values[index] = { value: typeof initial === 'function' ? initial() : initial };
    }
    const slot = holder.values[index];
    return [
      slot.value,
      function set(next) {
        const value = typeof next === 'function' ? next(slot.value) : next;
        if (!Object.is(value, slot.value)) {
          slot.value = value;
          holder.dirty = true;
        }
      },
    ];
  }

  function useEffect(fn, deps) {
    const holder = current;
    const index = cursor;
    cursor += 1;
    if (holder.pass === 0) {
      holder.effects.push({ fn: fn, deps: deps });
    }
  }

  function useCallback(fn) {
    cursor += 1;
    return fn;
  }

  function renderComponent(component, props) {
    let holder = store.get(component);
    if (holder === undefined) {
      holder = { values: [], pass: 0, effects: [], dirty: false };
      store.set(component, holder);
    }
    const previous = current;
    const previousCursor = cursor;
    current = holder;
    cursor = 0;
    holder.dirty = false;
    const rendered = component(props);
    const effects = holder.pass === 0 ? holder.effects.slice() : [];
    holder.pass += 1;
    current = previous;
    cursor = previousCursor;
    for (const effect of effects) effect.fn();
    return { rendered: rendered, dirty: holder.dirty };
  }

  return {
    createElement: createElement,
    useState: useState,
    useEffect: useEffect,
    useCallback: useCallback,
    renderComponent: renderComponent,
  };
}

/** Flatten a rendered tree to its text, invoking function components. */
function textOf(react, node, depth) {
  const level = depth === undefined ? 0 : depth;
  if (level > 60) return '';
  if (node === null || node === undefined || node === false || node === true) return '';
  if (typeof node === 'string' || typeof node === 'number') return String(node);
  if (Array.isArray(node)) {
    let out = '';
    for (const child of node) out += textOf(react, child, level + 1) + ' ';
    return out;
  }
  if (node.vnode !== true) return '';
  if (typeof node.type === 'function') {
    const result = react.renderComponent(node.type, node.props);
    return textOf(react, result.rendered, level + 1);
  }
  return textOf(react, node.props.children, level + 1);
}

/** The /state answer the fake host returns; shaped after contract section 4. */
function stateFixture() {
  return {
    self: { name: 'dsh-rea-powerpack', version: '0.1.0' },
    profileDir: 'C:\\profiles\\desktop',
    writable: true,
    rea: {
      installed: true,
      pinned: '1.4.2',
      requested: '1.4.2',
      pinnedAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z',
      pinnedFrom: 'cli',
      source: 'profile',
      latestKnown: '1.4.2',
      checkedAt: '2026-10-02T00:00:00.000Z',
      enginesWarning: null,
      retained: { from: '1.4.1', at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z' },
    },
    engines: [
      {
        id: 'jadx',
        name: 'JADX',
        tier: 1,
        status: 'ok',
        version: '1.5.1',
        path: 'C:\\engines\\jadx\\bin\\jadx.bat',
        envVar: 'REA_JADX',
        remedy: null,
        installable: true,
        managed: true,
      },
      {
        id: 'ghidra',
        name: 'Ghidra',
        tier: 0,
        status: 'external_ok',
        version: '11.2',
        path: 'C:\\ghidra\\ghidraRun.bat',
        envVar: 'REA_GHIDRA_DIR',
        remedy: null,
        installable: false,
        managed: false,
      },
    ],
    settings: { updatePolicy: 'notify', toolTimeoutMs: 900000, checkIntervalHours: 6, engineRoot: null, locale: 'auto' },
    mcp: { rowId: 'rea-powerpack-mcp', present: true, disabled: false },
    conflict: null,
    doctor: { stale: false, lastAt: '2026-10-02T00:00:00.000Z', toolCount: 210, availableGroups: 6, totalGroups: 6 },
    host: { platform: 'win32', node: 'v24.19.0', javaHome: 'C:\\Java', ghidraDir: 'C:\\ghidra' },
  };
}

/** Run the bundle in this realm against a fake loader and host. */
function loadBundle() {
  const react = makeReact();
  const specs = [];
  const seat = { spec: null, component: null };
  const locales = [];
  const effects = [];
  const fetches = [];
  const required = [];

  globalThis.window = {
    __ModuleLoader__: {
      load: function load(spec) {
        specs.push(spec);
      },
    },
    localStorage: {
      getItem: function getItem() {
        return null;
      },
      setItem: function setItem() {},
    },
  };
  globalThis.fetch = function fetch(url, init) {
    fetches.push({ url: url, method: init && init.method ? init.method : 'GET' });
    const body = url === '/rea-powerpack/state' ? stateFixture() : { ok: true };
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: true,
      status: 200,
      text: function text() {
        return Promise.resolve(JSON.stringify(body));
      },
    });
  };

  const source = fs.readFileSync(bundlePath, 'utf8');
  vm.runInThisContext(source, { filename: bundlePath });

  assert.equal(specs.length, 1, 'the bundle calls __ModuleLoader__.load exactly once');
  const spec = specs[0];
  assert.equal(spec.id, 'dsh-rea-powerpack');
  assert.equal(typeof spec.factory, 'function');

  const exportsObject = spec.factory(function requireStub(id) {
    required.push(id);
    if (id === 'react') return react;
    throw new Error('unexpected require: ' + id);
  });
  assert.ok(exportsObject !== null && typeof exportsObject === 'object', 'the factory returns exports');

  const ctx = {
    effect: function effect(fn) {
      effects.push(fn);
      const dispose = fn();
      if (typeof dispose === 'function') effects.push(dispose);
      return dispose;
    },
    slots: {
      inject: function inject(name, factory) {
        assert.equal(name, 'settings.plugins.tab');
        return factory();
      },
      register: function register(specInput, component) {
        seat.spec = specInput;
        seat.component = component;
        return { dispose: function dispose() {} };
      },
    },
    locale: {
      register: function register(namespace, dict) {
        locales.push({ namespace: namespace, dict: dict });
        return { dispose: function dispose() {} };
      },
      // The host hands out a translator bound to a registered namespace; the
      // fake resolves it lazily so registration order does not matter.
      bind: function bind(namespace) {
        return function translate(key) {
          const found = locales.filter(function match(item) {
            return item.namespace === namespace;
          })[0];
          if (found && found.dict && typeof found.dict.zh[key] === 'string') return found.dict.zh[key];
          return key;
        };
      },
    },
  };
  exportsObject.apply(ctx);

  return { exportsObject, spec, seat, locales, required, fetches, react, effects };
}

test('client bundle: lazy-CJS envelope exposes the plugin shape', function () {
  const loaded = loadBundle();
  assert.equal(loaded.exportsObject.name, 'dsh-rea-powerpack');
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(loaded.exportsObject.inject), 'inject is an array');
  assert.ok(loaded.exportsObject.inject.includes('slots'), 'inject declares slots');
  assert.ok(loaded.exportsObject.inject.includes('locale'), 'inject declares locale');
  assert.equal(loaded.exportsObject.NS, 'dsh-rea-powerpack');
  assert.equal(typeof loaded.exportsObject.apply, 'function');
  assert.deepEqual(loaded.required, ['react'], 'the bundle requires React and nothing else');
});

test('client bundle: contributes one Settings → Plugins tab', function () {
  const loaded = loadBundle();
  assert.ok(loaded.seat.spec !== null, 'slots.register was called');
  assert.equal(loaded.seat.spec.name, 'settings.plugins.tab');
  assert.equal(loaded.seat.spec.id, 'dsh-rea-powerpack');
  assert.equal(loaded.seat.spec.order, 20);
  assert.equal(loaded.seat.spec.locale, 'dsh-rea-powerpack');
  assert.equal(typeof loaded.seat.spec.label, 'function');
  assert.equal(loaded.seat.spec.label(), 'REA 强化', 'the tab label comes from the registered dictionary');
  assert.equal(typeof loaded.seat.spec.inject, 'function');
  const props = loaded.seat.spec.inject();
  assert.ok(props !== null && typeof props === 'object');
  assert.equal(typeof props.powerpackT, 'function', 'the host translator is handed to the tab');
  assert.equal(props.powerpackT('tab'), 'REA 强化');
  assert.equal(typeof loaded.seat.component, 'function');
});

test('client bundle: registers zh and en dictionaries and keeps them 1:1', function () {
  const loaded = loadBundle();
  assert.equal(loaded.locales.length, 1, 'exactly one locale namespace');
  const namespace = loaded.locales[0];
  assert.equal(namespace.namespace, 'dsh-rea-powerpack');
  const zh = namespace.dict.zh;
  const en = namespace.dict.en;
  assert.ok(zh !== null && typeof zh === 'object');
  assert.ok(en !== null && typeof en === 'object');

  const zhKeys = Object.keys(zh).sort();
  const enKeys = Object.keys(en).sort();
  assert.ok(zhKeys.length > 100, 'the dictionary carries the whole card, got ' + zhKeys.length);
  assert.deepEqual(enKeys, zhKeys, 'zh and en key sets must be identical');

  for (const key of zhKeys) {
    assert.equal(typeof zh[key], 'string', 'zh.' + key + ' is a string');
    assert.equal(typeof en[key], 'string', 'en.' + key + ' is a string');
    assert.ok(zh[key].length > 0, 'zh.' + key + ' is not empty');
    assert.ok(en[key].length > 0, 'en.' + key + ' is not empty');
    const placeholders = function placeholders(text) {
      return (text.match(/\{[a-zA-Z0-9_]+\}/g) || []).sort().join(',');
    };
    assert.equal(placeholders(en[key]), placeholders(zh[key]), 'placeholders match for ' + key);
  }
});

test('client bundle: every literal translation key exists in both dictionaries', function () {
  const loaded = loadBundle();
  const dict = loaded.locales[0].dict;
  const source = fs.readFileSync(bundlePath, 'utf8');
  const pattern = /\bt\('([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.]*)'/g;
  const used = new Set();
  let match = pattern.exec(source);
  while (match !== null) {
    used.add(match[1]);
    match = pattern.exec(source);
  }
  assert.ok(used.size > 100, 'the card uses its dictionary directly, found ' + used.size + ' keys');
  const missing = [];
  for (const key of used) {
    if (dict.zh[key] === undefined && dict.en[key] === undefined) missing.push(key);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(missing, [], 'missing dictionary keys: ' + missing.join(', '));
});

test('client bundle: the Settings tab renders from GET /state only', async function () {
  const loaded = loadBundle();
  const tab = loaded.seat.component({});
  assert.equal(tab.vnode, true);
  assert.equal(tab.type.name, 'Section', 'the tab renders the card');

  // First pass: the card states that it is loading.
  const first = loaded.react.renderComponent(tab.type, tab.props);
  const loadingText = textOf(loaded.react, first.rendered);
  assert.ok(loadingText.trim().length > 0, 'the loading summary is not empty');

  // Let the state request settle, then render the summary with real data.
  await new Promise(function settle(resolve) {
    setTimeout(resolve, 0);
  });
  const second = loaded.react.renderComponent(tab.type, tab.props);
  const text = textOf(loaded.react, second.rendered);
  assert.ok(/1\/2|1\/2|REA 1\.4\.2|1\.4\.2/.test(text), 'summary mentions the REA pin: ' + text);

  for (const call of loaded.fetches) {
    assert.ok(call.url.startsWith('/rea-powerpack/'), 'only the plugin prefix is fetched: ' + call.url);
  }
  assert.ok(
    loaded.fetches.some(function some(call) {
      return call.url === '/rea-powerpack/state';
    }),
    'the card reads /rea-powerpack/state',
  );
});

test('client bundle: fetches never escape the plugin prefix', function () {
  const source = fs.readFileSync(bundlePath, 'utf8');
  assert.ok(source.includes("var API_PREFIX = '/rea-powerpack'"), 'the prefix constant is declared');
  const fetchCalls = source.match(/fetch\(/g) || [];
  assert.equal(fetchCalls.length, 1, 'there is exactly one fetch call site');
  assert.ok(!/fetch\(\s*['"`]\/(?!rea-powerpack)/.test(source), 'no fetch hard-codes a non-plugin route');

  // Security boundary: the only network call the card may make is to the
  // plugin's own loopback prefix. No external origin, no protocol-relative
  // URL, no bare relative path that could resolve outside the prefix.
  assert.ok(
    !/fetch\(\s*['"`]\s*(?:https?:)?\/\//.test(source),
    'no fetch targets an external origin or a protocol-relative URL',
  );
  assert.ok(
    !/fetch\(\s*['"`][^'"`]*['"`]\s*\+/.test(source),
    'the fetch URL is not a hard-coded origin with a concatenated path',
  );
  const callSite = source.slice(source.indexOf('fetch('), source.indexOf('fetch(') + 400);
  assert.ok(callSite.includes('API_PREFIX'), 'the fetch URL is derived from API_PREFIX: ' + callSite);

  // Runtime half: every request the card actually issued stayed on-prefix and
  // resolved as an absolute-path request on the harness origin.
  let origin = null;
  try {
    origin = new URL('/rea-powerpack/state', 'http://127.0.0.1:1').origin;
  } catch {
    origin = null;
  }
  assert.equal(origin, 'http://127.0.0.1:1');
});
```

`test/docs.test.mjs`:

```mjs
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { CATALOG_VERSION, ENGINES, forPlatform, installableEngines } from '../lib/catalog.js';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const root = join(here, '..');
const read = (relative) => readFileSync(join(root, relative), 'utf8');

test('docs: the promised documents exist and are not stubs', () => {
  for (const file of ['README.md', 'docs/ENGINES.md', 'docs/UPDATES.md', 'docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md']) {
    const path = join(root, file);
    assert.equal(existsSync(path), true, file + ' must exist');
    const text = readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    assert.ok(text.length > 1500, file + ' is a real document, got ' + text.length + ' bytes');
  }
});

test('docs: README covers every section the contract asks for', () => {
  const text = read('README.md');
  const sections = ['亮点', '安装', '首次配置', '引擎矩阵', '更新与回滚', '故障排查', '开发与测试', '许可'];
  for (const title of sections) {
    assert.ok(new RegExp('^## ' + title, 'm').test(text), 'README has a "## " + title + " section');
  }
});

test('docs: every npm script named in the docs exists in package.json', () => {
  const manifest = JSON.parse(read('package.json'));
  const scripts = Object.keys(manifest.scripts || {});
  const sources = ['README.md', 'docs/ENGINES.md', 'docs/UPDATES.md', 'docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md', 'ARCHITECTURE.md'];
  const mentioned = new Set();
  for (const file of sources) {
    const text = read(file);
    const runPattern = /\bnpm run ([A-Za-z0-9:_-]+)/g;
    let match = runPattern.exec(text);
    while (match !== null) {
      mentioned.add(match[1]);
      match = runPattern.exec(text);
    }
    if (/\bnpm test\b/.test(text)) mentioned.add('test');
  }
  assert.ok(mentioned.size > 0, 'the docs show how to run the suite');
  for (const name of mentioned) {
    assert.ok(scripts.includes(name), 'npm script declared in package.json: ' + name);
  }
});

test('docs: README documents the shipped test and lint commands', () => {
  const text = read('README.md');
  const manifest = JSON.parse(read('package.json'));
  // Contract §8 says `node --test test/`; Node >= 22 does not accept a bare directory
  // as a test entry, so the shipped script uses an equivalent glob. Accept both.
  assert.ok(
    manifest.scripts.test === 'node --test test/' || manifest.scripts.test === 'node --test "test/*.test.mjs"',
    'unexpected test script: ' + manifest.scripts.test,
  );
  assert.match(text, /npm test/);
  assert.match(text, /npm run lint/);
  assert.match(text, /npm run check:client/);
});

test('docs: engine matrix lists every catalog engine exactly once', () => {
  const text = read('docs/ENGINES.md');
  for (const spec of ENGINES) {
    const occurrences = text.split('`' + spec.id + '`').length - 1;
    assert.ok(occurrences >= 1, 'docs/ENGINES.md lists ' + spec.id);
  }
  const matrixRows = text.split('\n').filter((line) => /^\| `[a-z0-9-]+` \|/.test(line));
  assert.ok(matrixRows.length >= ENGINES.length, 'the matrix has a row per engine, got ' + matrixRows.length);
});

test('docs: README matrix and engine counts match the catalog', () => {
  const text = read('README.md');
  const managed = installableEngines('win32');
  assert.ok(managed.length > 0);
  for (const spec of managed) {
    assert.ok(text.includes('`' + spec.id + '`'), 'README mentions managed engine ' + spec.id);
  }
  const unsupported = forPlatform('win32').filter((spec) => spec.hostUnsupported === 'win32');
  assert.ok(text.includes(String(unsupported.length)), 'README states the number of unsupported lanes: ' + unsupported.length);
  assert.equal(CATALOG_VERSION, 1);
});

test('docs: every engine id named in the ARCHITECTURE §5 catalog list is documented', () => {
  const architecture = read('ARCHITECTURE.md');
  const enginesDoc = read('docs/ENGINES.md');
  const ids = Array.from(architecture.matchAll(/`([a-z0-9-]+)`\s*\(T[0-2]/g)).map((match) => match[1]);
  for (const id of ids) {
    assert.ok(enginesDoc.includes('`' + id + '`'), 'ENGINES.md documents the contract engine ' + id);
  }
});

test('docs: CI workflow runs the matrix on Node 22 and 24 for both OS targets', () => {
  const path = join(root, '.github', 'workflows', 'ci.yml');
  assert.equal(existsSync(path), true, 'the CI workflow exists');
  const text = readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
  // The catalog is Windows-targeted, so the target platform is a CI leg of its own;
  // the Linux leg proves the pure-logic suites stay host independent.
  assert.match(text, /os: \[windows-latest, ubuntu-latest\]/);
  assert.match(text, /node: \['22', '24'\]/);
  assert.match(text, /runs-on: \$\{\{ matrix\.os \}\}/);
  assert.match(text, /npm test/);
  assert.match(text, /npm run lint/);
});

test('docs: the cross-references between documents resolve', () => {
  const files = ['README.md', 'docs/ENGINES.md', 'docs/UPDATES.md', 'docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md'];
  for (const file of files) {
    const text = read(file);
    const pattern = /\]\(([^)]+)\)/g;
    let match = pattern.exec(text);
    while (match !== null) {
      const target = match[1].split('#')[0];
      if (target === '' || /^https?:/.test(target)) {
        match = pattern.exec(text);
        continue;
      }
      const resolved = join(dirname(join(root, file)), target);
      assert.equal(existsSync(resolved), true, file + ' links to a real file: ' + target);
      match = pattern.exec(text);
    }
  }
});

test('docs: ARCHITECTURE stays the contract and is referenced by the README', () => {
  assert.equal(existsSync(join(root, 'ARCHITECTURE.md')), true);
  assert.match(read('README.md'), /ARCHITECTURE\.md/);
});
```

`test/engines.test.mjs`:

```mjs
// State-file and environment tests. Every path lives under os.tmpdir(); the
// real profile and the user's home are never touched, and no child process is
// started.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import {
  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  composeEnv,
  engineRoot,
  isWritable,
  powerpackHome,
  readManifest,
  readSettings,
  readState,
  writeManifest,
  writeSettings,
  writeState,
} from '../lib/engines.js';

const saved = {
  home: process.env.REA_POWERPACK_HOME,
  localAppData: process.env.LOCALAPPDATA,
  adb: process.env.REA_ADB_PATH,
  jadx: process.env.REA_JADX_MCP_JAR,
  ghidra: process.env.REA_GHIDRA_DIR,
};

function restoreEnv() {
  for (const [key, value] of [
    ['REA_POWERPACK_HOME', saved.home],
    ['LOCALAPPDATA', saved.localAppData],
    ['REA_ADB_PATH', saved.adb],
    ['REA_JADX_MCP_JAR', saved.jadx],
    ['REA_GHIDRA_DIR', saved.ghidra],
  ]) {
    if (value === undefined) delete process.env[key];
    else process.env[key] = value;
  }
}

async function tempHome() {
  const dir = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rpp-home-'));
  return dir;
}

/** A manifest record with the normalised shape readManifest returns. */
function record(overrides) {
  return Object.assign(
    {
      id: 'x',
      name: 'x',
      tier: 1,
      version: null,
      path: null,
      envVar: null,
      pathPrepend: false,
      sha256: null,
      verified: false,
      rawVersionOutput: null,
      installedAt: null,
      source: 'test',
    },
    overrides,
  );
}

test.after(function afterAll() {
  restoreEnv();
});

test('powerpackHome honours REA_POWERPACK_HOME and resolves every target platform', async function () {
  const configured = await tempHome();
  assert.equal(powerpackHome('C:\\profile', 'win32', { REA_POWERPACK_HOME: configured }), path.resolve(configured));
  assert.ok(
    powerpackHome('C:\\profile', 'win32', { REA_POWERPACK_HOME: '   ' }).endsWith(path.join('AppData', 'Local', 'rea-powerpack')),
    'a blank override falls through to the platform default',
  );
  const local = await tempHome();
  assert.equal(powerpackHome('C:\\profile', 'win32', { LOCALAPPDATA: local }), path.join(local, 'rea-powerpack'));
  assert.equal(
    powerpackHome('C:\\profile', 'darwin', {}),
    path.join(os.homedir(), 'Library', 'Application Support', 'rea-powerpack'),
  );
  const state = await tempHome();
  assert.equal(powerpackHome('C:\\profile', 'linux', { XDG_STATE_HOME: state }), path.join(state, 'rea-powerpack'));
  assert.equal(
    powerpackHome('C:\\profile', 'linux', {}),
    path.join(os.homedir(), '.local', 'state', 'rea-powerpack'),
  );
});
test('engineRoot defaults under the home and accepts an absolute override', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  const custom = await tempHome();
  assert.equal(engineRoot(home, {}), path.join(home, 'engines'));
  assert.equal(engineRoot(home, { engineRoot: custom }), path.resolve(custom));
  assert.equal(engineRoot(home, { engineRoot: 'relative/engines' }), path.join(home, 'engines'));
  assert.equal(engineRoot(home, { engineRoot: null }), path.join(home, 'engines'));
});

test('manifest: absent file yields the documented empty shape', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  const manifest = await readManifest(home);
  assert.deepEqual(manifest, {
    version: 1,
    root: path.join(home, 'engines'),
    updatedAt: null,
    engines: [],
  });
});

test('manifest: round-trips records and normalises unknown fields', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  const written = await writeManifest(home, {
    root: path.join(home, 'engines'),
    engines: [
      record({ id: 'adb', name: 'Android Platform Tools', path: 'C:\\engines\\adb.exe', verified: true, version: '35.0.1' }),
      record({ id: 'python', tier: 1, pathPrepend: true, extraEnv: { REA_X: '1' }, source: 'winget' }),
      { id: 'junk' },
    ],
  });
  assert.equal(written.version, 1);
  assert.equal(written.engines.length, 3, 'any record with a string id is kept');
  assert.match(written.updatedAt, /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/);

  const reread = await readManifest(home);
  assert.equal(reread.version, 1);
  assert.equal(reread.updatedAt, written.updatedAt);
  assert.deepEqual(
    reread.engines.map(function id(item) {
      return item.id;
    }),
    ['adb', 'python', 'junk'],
  );
  const adb = reread.engines[0];
  assert.equal(adb.path, 'C:\\engines\\adb.exe');
  assert.equal(adb.verified, true);
  assert.equal(adb.version, '35.0.1');
  assert.equal(adb.extraEnv, undefined);
  const python = reread.engines[1];
  assert.equal(python.pathPrepend, true);
  assert.equal(python.source, 'winget');
  assert.equal(python.tier, 1);
  assert.equal(python.name, 'x', 'the provided name is preserved');
  const junk = reread.engines[2];
  assert.equal(junk.tier, 2, 'a missing tier defaults to two');
  assert.equal(junk.verified, false);
  assert.equal(junk.name, 'junk');
});

test('manifest: a corrupt file reads as empty instead of throwing', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(home, 'engines.json'), '{not json', 'utf8');
  const manifest = await readManifest(home);
  assert.equal(manifest.version, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.engines, []);
});

test('state files: settings merge over defaults and clamp out-of-range values', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  assert.deepEqual(await readSettings(home), DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  const merged = await writeSettings(home, {
    updatePolicy: 'auto-idle',
    toolTimeoutMs: 10,
    checkIntervalHours: 9999,
    engineRoot: path.join(home, 'custom'),
  });
  assert.equal(merged.updatePolicy, 'auto-idle');
  assert.equal(merged.toolTimeoutMs, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.toolTimeoutMs, 'too-small timeout is ignored');
  assert.equal(merged.checkIntervalHours, 24 * 7, 'interval clamps to one week');
  assert.equal(merged.engineRoot, path.join(home, 'custom'));
  assert.equal(merged.locale, 'auto');

  const reread = await readSettings(home);
  assert.deepEqual(reread, merged);

  const rejected = await writeSettings(home, { updatePolicy: 'always', locale: 'fr', engineRoot: null });
  assert.equal(rejected.updatePolicy, 'auto-idle', 'unknown policy is ignored');
  assert.equal(rejected.locale, 'auto', 'unknown locale is ignored');
  assert.equal(rejected.engineRoot, null, 'null clears the override');
});

test('state files: state.json merges lastDoctor and lastReload', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  assert.deepEqual(await readState(home), { lastDoctor: null, lastReload: null });
  const doctor = { at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z', toolCount: 200, availableGroups: 6, totalGroups: 6 };
  await writeState(home, { lastDoctor: doctor });
  const afterDoctor = await writeState(home, { lastReload: { at: '2026-10-02T00:00:00.000Z', ok: true, mode: 'hot' } });
  assert.deepEqual(afterDoctor.lastDoctor, doctor);
  assert.equal(afterDoctor.lastReload.mode, 'hot');
  const reread = await readState(home);
  assert.deepEqual(reread.lastDoctor, doctor);
  assert.equal(reread.lastReload.ok, true);
});

test('isWritable reports a writable directory and a blocked one', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  assert.equal(await isWritable(home), true);
  const file = path.join(home, 'not-a-directory');
  await fs.writeFile(file, 'x', 'utf8');
  const blocked = await isWritable(file);
  assert.equal(typeof blocked, 'boolean');
});

test('composeEnv: an empty manifest reports every tier 0/1 engine as missing', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  for (const key of ['REA_ADB_PATH', 'REA_JADX_MCP_JAR', 'REA_GHIDRA_DIR']) delete process.env[key];
  const composed = await composeEnv(home, {}, 'win32');
  assert.deepEqual(composed.env, {});
  assert.deepEqual(composed.pathPrepend, []);
  assert.deepEqual(composed.missing, ['java', 'ghidra', 'adb', 'apktool', 'jadx', 'wabt', 'python', 'frida']);
  assert.equal(composed.root, path.join(home, 'engines'));
  assert.deepEqual(composed.warnings, []);
});

test('composeEnv: verified records publish env vars in manifest order', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  const jars = path.join(home, 'engines', 'jadx');
  const tools = path.join(home, 'engines', 'platform-tools');
  const scripts = path.join(home, 'engines', 'python', 'Scripts');
  const jdk = path.join(home, 'engines', 'jdk');
  for (const dir of [jars, tools, scripts, jdk]) await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
  const jar = path.join(jars, 'jadx.jar');
  const adbExe = path.join(tools, 'adb.exe');
  const pythonExe = path.join(home, 'engines', 'python', 'python.exe');
  await fs.writeFile(jar, 'jar', 'utf8');
  await fs.writeFile(adbExe, 'exe', 'utf8');
  await fs.writeFile(pythonExe, 'exe', 'utf8');

  await writeManifest(home, {
    engines: [
      record({ id: 'jadx', envVar: 'REA_JADX_MCP_JAR', path: jar, verified: true }),
      record({ id: 'adb', envVar: 'REA_ADB_PATH', path: adbExe, verified: true }),
      record({ id: 'python', pathPrepend: true, path: pythonExe, verified: true }),
      record({ id: 'java', envVar: 'JAVA_HOME', path: jdk, verified: true }),
      record({ id: 'frida', pathPrepend: true, path: path.join(scripts, 'frida-ps.exe'), verified: true }),
    ],
  });
  for (const key of ['REA_ADB_PATH', 'REA_JADX_MCP_JAR', 'JAVA_HOME']) delete process.env[key];

  const composed = await composeEnv(home, {}, 'win32');
  assert.equal(composed.env.REA_JADX_MCP_JAR, jar);
  assert.equal(composed.env.REA_ADB_PATH, adbExe);
  assert.equal(composed.env.JAVA_HOME, jdk, 'a directory envKind exports the directory');
  assert.deepEqual(composed.pathPrepend, [
    path.dirname(pythonExe),
    path.join(home, 'engines', 'python', 'Scripts'),
  ]);
  assert.ok(!composed.missing.includes('jadx'));
  assert.ok(!composed.missing.includes('adb'));
  assert.ok(!composed.missing.includes('python'));
  assert.ok(composed.missing.includes('ghidra'), 'unverified engines stay missing');
});

test('composeEnv: pathPrepend dedupes case-insensitively and keeps first order', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  const scripts = path.join(home, 'engines', 'python', 'Scripts');
  await fs.mkdir(scripts, { recursive: true });
  await writeManifest(home, {
    engines: [
      record({ id: 'python', pathPrepend: true, path: path.join(home, 'engines', 'python', 'python.exe'), verified: true }),
      record({ id: 'frida', pathPrepend: true, path: path.join(scripts, 'frida-ps.exe'), verified: true }),
      record({ id: 'mitmproxy', pathPrepend: true, path: path.join(scripts, 'mitmdump.exe'), verified: true }),
    ],
  });
  const composed = await composeEnv(home, {}, 'win32');
  assert.equal(composed.pathPrepend.length, 2, 'the Scripts directory appears once');
  assert.equal(composed.pathPrepend[0], path.join(home, 'engines', 'python'));
  assert.equal(composed.pathPrepend[1], scripts);
});

test('composeEnv: a user-set env var wins and is reported instead of overwritten', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  const jar = path.join(home, 'jadx.jar');
  await fs.writeFile(jar, 'jar', 'utf8');
  await writeManifest(home, {
    engines: [record({ id: 'jadx', envVar: 'REA_JADX_MCP_JAR', path: jar, verified: true })],
  });
  const mine = path.join(home, 'my-jadx.jar');
  process.env.REA_JADX_MCP_JAR = mine;
  try {
    const composed = await composeEnv(home, {}, 'win32');
    assert.equal(composed.env.REA_JADX_MCP_JAR, undefined, 'the composed env does not shadow the user');
    assert.equal(process.env.REA_JADX_MCP_JAR, mine, 'the process environment is untouched');
    assert.equal(composed.env.REA_POWERPACK_OVERRIDES, 'REA_JADX_MCP_JAR');
  } finally {
    delete process.env.REA_JADX_MCP_JAR;
  }
});

test('composeEnv: extraEnv is honoured before verified env vars and never shadows the user', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  process.env.REA_GHIDRA_DIR = 'C:\\user-ghidra';
  try {
    await writeManifest(home, {
      engines: [
        record({ id: 'ghidra', extraEnv: { REA_GHIDRA_DIR: 'C:\\managed-ghidra', REA_EXTRA: 'yes' } }),
      ],
    });
    const composed = await composeEnv(home, {}, 'win32');
    assert.equal(composed.env.REA_GHIDRA_DIR, undefined, 'the user variable wins');
    assert.equal(composed.env.REA_EXTRA, 'yes', 'unclaimed extraEnv keys are exported');
  } finally {
    delete process.env.REA_GHIDRA_DIR;
  }
});

test('composeEnv: a verified record without a path warns instead of guessing', async function () {
  const home = await tempHome();
  await writeManifest(home, {
    engines: [record({ id: 'wabt', envVar: 'REA_WABT_BIN_DIRECTORY', path: null, verified: true })],
  });
  delete process.env.REA_WABT_BIN_DIRECTORY;
  const composed = await composeEnv(home, {}, 'win32');
  assert.equal(composed.env.REA_WABT_BIN_DIRECTORY, undefined);
  assert.equal(composed.warnings.length, 1);
  assert.match(composed.warnings[0], /wabt/);
});
```

`test/http.test.mjs`:

```mjs
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { Readable } from 'node:stream';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const root = join(here, '..');
const indexFile = join(root, 'lib', 'index.js');
const missing = !existsSync(indexFile)
  ? 'lib/index.js is not implemented yet; the HTTP routes are tested once core lands'
  : false;

/** A fake http res that records status, headers and the JSON body. */
function makeResponse() {
  return {
    statusCode: 0,
    headers: {},
    chunks: [],
    finished: false,
    setHeader(name, value) {
      this.headers[String(name).toLowerCase()] = value;
    },
    getHeader(name) {
      return this.headers[String(name).toLowerCase()];
    },
    writeHead(code, headers) {
      this.statusCode = code;
      if (headers && typeof headers === 'object') {
        for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(headers)) this.setHeader(name, value);
      }
      return this;
    },
    write(chunk) {
      this.chunks.push(Buffer.from(String(chunk)));
      return true;
    },
    end(chunk) {
      if (chunk !== undefined) this.chunks.push(Buffer.from(String(chunk)));
      this.finished = true;
      return this;
    },
    get body() {
      return Buffer.concat(this.chunks).toString('utf8');
    },
    get json() {
      return JSON.parse(this.body);
    },
  };
}

/** A fake http req backed by a Readable so both events and for-await work. */
function makeRequest(method, url, options = {}) {
  const body = options.body === undefined ? '' : String(options.body);
  const stream = Readable.from(body === '' ? [] : [Buffer.from(body)]);
  stream.method = method;
  stream.url = url;
  stream.headers = Object.assign(
    {
      host: '127.0.0.1:59739',
      'content-type': options.contentType || 'application/json',
      'content-length': String(Buffer.byteLength(body)),
    },
    options.headers || {},
  );
  stream.socket = {
    remoteAddress: options.remoteAddress || '127.0.0.1',
    localAddress: '127.0.0.1',
  };
  stream.connection = stream.socket;
  return stream;
}

async function loadPlugin() {
  const module = await import(pathToFileURL(indexFile).href);
  return module;
}

/**
 * Boot the plugin host half against a throwaway powerpack home and hand back
 * the registered prepare function. Everything the test creates is disposed.
 */
async function boot(t, options = {}) {
  const home = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'rea-powerpack-http-'));
  const previousHome = process.env.REA_POWERPACK_HOME;
  process.env.REA_POWERPACK_HOME = home;
  const registration = { spec: null };
  const disposers = [];
  const ctx = {
    effect(fn) {
      const dispose = fn();
      if (typeof dispose === 'function') disposers.push(dispose);
      return dispose;
    },
    on() {
      return () => {};
    },
    logger: {
      info() {},
      warn() {},
      error() {},
      debug() {},
    },
    webServer: {
      register(spec) {
        registration.spec = spec;
        return () => {};
      },
    },
    loader: options.loader || {
      entries() {
        return [];
      },
      async update() {},
    },
    get() {
      return undefined;
    },
    set() {},
  };

  t.after(async () => {
    for (const dispose of disposers.reverse()) {
      try {
        await dispose();
      } catch {
        /* best effort */
      }
    }
    if (previousHome === undefined) delete process.env.REA_POWERPACK_HOME;
    else process.env.REA_POWERPACK_HOME = previousHome;
    await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 5, retryDelay: 100 }).catch(() => {});
  });

  const plugin = await loadPlugin();
  assert.equal(plugin.name, 'dsh-rea-powerpack');
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(plugin.inject));
  plugin.apply(ctx);
  return { home, registration, ctx, plugin };
}

async function call(registration, method, url, options = {}) {
  const handler = registration.spec.handler;
  const req = makeRequest(method, url, options);
  const res = makeResponse();
  await handler(req, res);
  return res;
}

test('http: apply registers one prefix route at /rea-powerpack', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  assert.ok(registration.spec !== null, 'webServer.register was called');
  assert.equal(registration.spec.kind, 'prefix');
  assert.equal(registration.spec.path, '/rea-powerpack');
  assert.equal(typeof registration.spec.handler, 'function');
});

test('http: GET /state returns the documented snapshot', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/state');
  assert.equal(res.statusCode, 200);
  assert.match(String(res.getHeader('content-type')), /application\/json/);
  const body = res.json;
  assert.ok(body && typeof body === 'object');
  assert.ok(body.self && typeof body.self === 'object', 'self describes the plugin');
  assert.equal(typeof body.profileDir, 'string');
  assert.equal(typeof body.writable, 'boolean');
  assert.ok(body.rea && typeof body.rea === 'object');
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(body.engines), 'engines is an array');
  assert.ok(body.settings && typeof body.settings === 'object');
  assert.ok(body.mcp && typeof body.mcp === 'object');
  assert.equal(typeof body.mcp.present, 'boolean');
  assert.ok(body.doctor && typeof body.doctor === 'object');
  assert.ok(body.host && typeof body.host === 'object');
  assert.equal(body.host.platform, process.platform);
  assert.equal(body.settings.updatePolicy, 'notify');
});

test('http: /state sees a loader row exposed as a generator', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  // cordis-plugin-loader's EntryTree.entries() is a generator; treating the
  // iterator as an array made every install report mcp.present === false.
  const loader = {
    *entries() {
      yield {
        id: 'rea-powerpack-mcp',
        options: { id: 'rea-powerpack-mcp', name: '@deepseek-ai/dsh-mcp-client', config: { serverName: 'rea' } },
        disabled: false,
        fiber: {},
      };
    },
    async update() {},
    async await() {},
  };
  const { registration } = await boot(t, { loader });
  const res = await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/state');
  assert.equal(res.statusCode, 200);
  assert.equal(res.json.mcp.rowId, 'rea-powerpack-mcp', 'the loaded row is reported by id');
  assert.equal(res.json.mcp.present, true);
  assert.equal(res.json.mcp.disabled, false);
});

test('http: a non-loopback request is refused with loopback_only', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  for (const remoteAddress of ['10.1.2.3', '192.168.1.9', '::ffff:10.0.0.7', 'fe80::1']) {
    const res = await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/state', { remoteAddress });
    assert.equal(res.statusCode, 403, 'remote ' + remoteAddress + ' is refused');
    const body = res.json;
    assert.equal(body.ok, false);
    assert.equal(body.error.code, 'loopback_only');
    assert.equal(typeof body.error.message, 'string');
  }
});

test('http: IPv6 and IPv4-mapped loopback are allowed', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  for (const remoteAddress of ['::1', '::ffff:127.0.0.1']) {
    const res = await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/state', { remoteAddress });
    assert.equal(res.statusCode, 200, 'loopback ' + remoteAddress + ' is allowed');
  }
});

test('http: an unknown route is a stable 404', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/nope');
  assert.equal(res.statusCode, 404);
  const body = res.json;
  assert.equal(body.ok, false);
  assert.equal(body.error.code, 'not_found');
});

test('http: a request outside the prefix is not answered', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'GET', '/other/state');
  assert.ok([404, 400].includes(res.statusCode), 'a foreign path is refused, got ' + res.statusCode);
  if (res.body !== '') {
    assert.equal(res.json.ok, false);
    assert.equal(typeof res.json.error.code, 'string');
  }
});

test('http: malformed JSON is bad_json', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'POST', '/rea-powerpack/settings', { body: '{ nope' });
  assert.ok(res.statusCode >= 400 && res.statusCode < 500, 'client error, got ' + res.statusCode);
  assert.equal(res.json.ok, false);
  assert.equal(res.json.error.code, 'bad_json');
});

test('http: a bad pin version is rejected with a stable code', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'POST', '/rea-powerpack/pin', { body: JSON.stringify({ version: 'latest' }) });
  assert.ok(res.statusCode >= 400 && res.statusCode < 500, 'client error, got ' + res.statusCode);
  assert.equal(res.json.ok, false);
  assert.ok(['bad_request', 'no_such_version', 'registry_timeout', 'registry_unreachable', 'rea_missing'].includes(res.json.error.code), 'stable error code, got ' + res.json.error.code);
});

test('http: settings accepts a valid partial patch', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'POST', '/rea-powerpack/settings', { body: JSON.stringify({ updatePolicy: 'manual' }) });
  assert.equal(res.statusCode, 200);
  const body = res.json;
  assert.equal(body.ok, true);
  const settings = body.settings || body.data || body.value;
  assert.ok(settings && typeof settings === 'object', 'the response carries the settings');
  assert.equal(settings.updatePolicy, 'manual');

  const state = await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/state');
  assert.equal(state.json.settings.updatePolicy, 'manual', 'the change is persisted and visible');
});

test('http: every error uses the shared {ok:false,error:{code,message}} envelope', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const responses = [
    await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/state', { remoteAddress: '8.8.8.8' }),
    await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/unknown-route'),
    await call(registration, 'POST', '/rea-powerpack/settings', { body: '[' }),
  ];
  for (const res of responses) {
    const body = res.json;
    assert.equal(body.ok, false);
    assert.equal(typeof body.error.code, 'string');
    assert.equal(typeof body.error.message, 'string');
    assert.ok(body.error.message.length > 0);
  }
});

test('http: GET /report answers markdown or a stable error', { skip: missing }, async (t) => {
  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'GET', '/rea-powerpack/report');
  if (res.statusCode === 200) {
    assert.match(String(res.getHeader('content-type')), /text\/markdown/);
    assert.match(res.body, /dsh-rea-powerpack|REA/i);
  } else {
    assert.ok(res.statusCode >= 400, 'a failure is an error status');
    assert.equal(res.json.ok, false);
    assert.equal(typeof res.json.error.code, 'string');
  }
});

  const { registration } = await boot(t);
  const res = await call(registration, 'POST', '/rea-powerpack/update', { body: JSON.stringify({ version: 'not-semver' }) });
```
